kāycci urukkiṉum māṟṟum niṟaiyum kuṉṟā teṉpataṟ kaṟi kuṟiyāka, atikārikaḷāl tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭa tuḷaippoṉ eṉpatum, akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. 2 tūya veḷḷikkācum tuḷaiyiṭappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
mutaṟkālattil akamatippum puṟamatippum ottirunta nāṇa yaṅkaḷ cila piṟkālattil niṟai kuṟaintum tāḻnta tātu kalantum kuṟināṇayam (Token coins) āyiṉa.
caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kācukaḷil, aracar kula muttirai yōṭu avvav varacar ciṟappup peyarum vaḻipaṭu teyvakkuṟiyum piṟa vaṭivaṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. avai avaṟṟai aṭippitta aracar peyarāl maturāntakaṉ māṭai  vīrapāṇṭiyaṉ kācu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉa. muttirai maṭṭumuḷḷa paḻaṅkācukaḷ paḻampāṇ ṭiyaṉ kācu, paḻañcōḻaṉ kācu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. pēraracar kācukaḷil avarāl vellappaṭṭa aracar muttiraikaḷum uṭaṉpoṟikkap paṭṭaṉa. cila kācukaḷ irupuṟamum orē vakaiyākavum, cila vēṟu vakaiyākavum kuṟikaḷ koṇṭiruntaṉa. kācaṭippataṟkut taṉivari vāṅkappaṭṭatu. atu aṭikācu poṉvari māṭaikkūli mutaliya peyarkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
mūvēntar kācukaḷum, mutalāvatu caturamākavum nīḷ caturamākavu miruntu piṉṉar vaṭṭavaṭivu peṟṟaṉa veṉṟu, nāṇayavārāycci yāḷar kūṟuvar.
piṟavaracar kācukaḷ: ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntu, vaṇikat toṭarpākavum araciyal toṭarpākavum piṟavaracar kācukaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
palakāla rōmakkācukaḷ - ciṟappāka 4ām 5ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟkuriyavai-tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum, avaṟṟuḷḷum ciṟappāka maturaiyaic cuṟṟiyum, mūvulōkattilum peruvāri yāyk kiṭait tirukkiṉṟaṉa. mutaṟ pāṇṭiyap pēraracukālattil (590-925)tirupputtūriluḷḷa oru pārppaṉiyār avvūrk kōyilil oru viḷakkēṟṟutaṟku 10 tiṉāram 3(rōmap poṟkācu), 
aḷittaṉar. caṭāvarmaṉ kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉ (1190-1217), taṉ peyar tāṅkiya kuḷam  oṉṟai āḻamākkutaṟku 100 tirammam 4 (kirēkkap poṟkācu) koṭuttāṉ. itaṉāl yavaṉak kācukaḷ aracarālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭamai aṟiyalākum.
pallavar mēmpaṭṭirunta iṭaikkālattil, avaruṭaiya kāḷai muttiraiyum viḷakku cakkaram mutaliya piṟa kuṟikaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭa kācukaḷ, cōḻanāṭu muḻumaiyum paraviyiruntaṉa.
12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vīrapāṇṭiyaṉukkum kulacēkara pāṇṭiyaṉukkum naṭanta uḷnāṭṭup pōril, muṉṉavaṉukkut tuṇaiyāyirunta mutalām parākkiramapāku eṉṉum ilaṅkai yaracaṉukkup pāṇṭināṭṭil ēṟpaṭṭa ātikkattiṉāl, avaṉatu kākap paṇam eṉṉum īḻakkācu ciṟitukālam teṉṉāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. atu caturamāṉatum, 72 allatu 73 kuṉṟimaṇi eṭaiyuḷḷatum, oru muraṭṭu māntaṉ oru puṟattil niṟpatum oru puṟattil iruppatum pōlap poṟittatum, aracu (pōti) yāṉai cakkaram kumpam mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷaik koṇṭatumāṉa ceppuk kācākum.
cāḷukkiyarkkut tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭapākattila celvāk kēṟpaṭṭa piṟkālattil, avarkaḷatu varāka muttirai koṇṭatum 
3 1/2 rūpā matippuḷḷatumāṉa varākaṉ eṉṉum poṟkācu, tamiḻnāṭu muḻumaiyum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ṭilliyiruntu āṇṭa ṭōkḷāk 
(1325-50) eṉṉum mukamatiyap pēraracaṉiṉ taṅkar eṉṉum ceppukkācu, teṉṉāṭuvarai vaḻaṅkiyatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iṅṅaṉam, iṟutikkālattil, mūvēntar kācukaḷiṭaiyē vēṟu pallaracar kācukaḷum viravi vaḻaṅkiyiruntirukkiṉṟaṉa.
13
maṟamum pōrum
nāṭukāval naṉmai ceytal porutal ākiya mūvakai araca viṉaikaḷuḷ, iṟutiyatu aracarkkuc ciṟantatātaliṉ, atu viṉaiyeṉṉum potuccollāl vitantu kuṟikkap peṟuvatu marapu.
aracar pōr ācai, pātukāppu, pakai, taṇṭaṉai, maṟam, aruḷ eṉṉum aṟuvakaik kāraṇattāṟ piṟakkum.
maṇṇācai, peṇṇācai, poṉṉācai, pukaḻācai, viṇṇācai eṉa ācai aivakaippaṭum. taṟkāppu, nāṭṭukkāppu, matakkāppu eṉap pātukāppu mūvakaippaṭum. paḻitīrppu, paḻampakai, poṟāmai eṉap pakai mūvakaippaṭum. piṟavaracariṉ ikaḻccipaṟṟiyatum tiṟaiyiṟāmai paṟṟiyatum paṭaiyeṭuppup paṟṟiyatum eṉat taṇṭaṉai mūvakaippaṭum. maṟam oṉṟē. atu kaṭumaṟam. taṉ kuṭikaḷmītu koṇṭatum piṟar kuṭikaḷmītu koṇṭatum eṉa aruḷ iruvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu, kaṭalkōḷ nērntaviṭattu vāḻ nilamillāk kuṭikaṭkup piṟar nāṭṭup pakutikaḷai veṉṟu taruvatu pōṉṟatu; piṉṉatu, koṭuṅkōl maṉṉar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu avar kuṭikaḷai mīṭpatu pōṉṟatu. iṉi, ōr aracaṉ iṟantapiṉ nēr piṟaṅkaṭai yillāviṭattu nikaḻum tāyappōrum uṇṭu. atu taṉṉurimai kāppatākaliṉ taṟkāppiṉpāṟpaṭum.
aracar tam pakaiyaracar tiṟantu naṭantukoḷḷum muṟai iṉcol, pirippu, kūṭṭu, koṭai, uṭaṉpaṭikkai, cantu, taṇṭaṉai, acaiviṉmai, ākulam, araṭṭu, vañcaṉai, oḷivu, pukalaṭaivu, maṟpōr, kalaippōr eṉap patiṉaintu tiṟattatu. pirippeṉpatu, pakaiyaracariṉ tuṇaivarai avariṉiṉṟu, pirittuviṭuvatu. kūṭṭu eṉpatu, pakaiyaracariṉ tuṇaivarait tammoṭu kūṭṭik koḷvatum, piṟavaracarait tammoṭu kūṭṭik koḷvatum eṉa iruvakaiyatu. cantu eṉpatu, iru pakaiyaracarai mūṉṟām aracaṉ icaittu vaippatu. taṇṭaṉai eṉpatu, pōr. acaiviṉmai yeṉpatu, pakaiyaip poruṭpaṭuttātu vāḷā viruttal. ākulam eṉpatu, āravāram. atu toṇṭaimāṉ auvaiyārkkut taṉ paṭaikkalak koṭṭilaik kāṭṭiyatum, pōrkkaḷattil pakaivar añcumāṟu paṭaikaḷai aṇivakuttu niṟuttiyatum pōṉṟatu. araṭṭu eṉpatu, neṭumoḻi allatu vañciṉam. vañcaṉaiyeṉpatu, maṟaintiruntu tākkuvatum, mūvēn tarum pāriyaiyum muttinātaṉ meypporuḷ nāyaṉāraiyum vañcittuk koṉṟatum pōṉṟatu. oḷivu eṉpatu, malaiyiṟpōy oḷintukoḷvatu. pukalaṭaivu eṉpatu, vēṟṟaracaṉiṭam ceṉṟu aṭaikkalam pukuvatu. maṟpōr eṉpatu, paṭaivaliyāl vella viyalāta pōtu māṟṟaracaṉai maṟpōrāl velvatu. kalaippōr eṉpatu, irāvaṇaṉ teṉṉāṭṭiṟ pukātavāṟu akattiyar pāṇṭiyaṉ cārpāka avaṉai icaiyāṟ piṇittatu pōlvatu. paḻaṅkālattil, ōr aracaṉ taṉ pakaivaṉaip pōril vella muṭiyāviṭiṉ, avaṉai vēṟoru kalaiyil velvatum  ciṟupāṉmai nikaḻntu vantatu. piṟa veḷippaṭai.
paṇṭaikkālattil pōrviṉai, veṭci karantai vañci tumpai uḻiñai nocci kāñci eṉa eḻuvakaip paṭṭiruntatu. av veḻuvakaiyum muṟaiyē, veṭci karantai mutaliya eḻuvakaip pūkkaḷ allatu pūmālaikaḷ cūṭip porappaṭṭamaiyāl, ap peyar peṟṟaṉa. veṭci eṉpatu pakaivar allatu vēṟṟaracar nāṭ ṭiluḷḷa āniraikaḷaik kavarvatu. itu oru nāṭṭu maṟavar tāmē kavarvatum, tam aracaṉāl ēvappaṭṭuk kavarvatum, eṉa iruvakai. ivaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉatu taṉṉuṟu toḻil eṉṟum, piṉṉatu maṉṉuṟu toḻil eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. karantaiyeṉpatu kavarappaṭṭa āniraikaḷai mīṭṭal. vañciyeṉpatu, vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭaiyeṭuttuc cellal. tumpai yeṉpatu, oru veḷi nilattiṟ porutal. uḻiñai yeṉpatu, ōr aracaṉ vēṟṟaracaṉ nakarai muṟṟukaiyiṭal. nocciyeṉpatu, muṟṟukai yiṭappaṭṭavaṉ taṉ nakaraik kāttal. kāñciyeṉpatu, nilaiyāmai viḷaṅkit tōṉṟumpaṭi eṇṇiṟantavar viraintu paṭumāṟu irupaṭaikaḷum ūṉṟip porutal.
pōr ceytaṟku, aracaṉ nāṭ pārttalum avaṉuṭaiya maṟavar kuṟipārttalum marapu. aracaṉ peruṅ kaṇiyai viṉavi nalla nāṭkuṟittu, atil paṭaiyoṭu pōrukkup puṟappaṭa vacatiyillā viṭiṉ, aṉṟu taṉ kuṭaiyaiyum vāḷaiyum kōṭṭai vāyilukku veḷiyē pōkki vaippatu vaḻakkam. itu kuṭaināṭkōḷ eṉṟum, vāḷnāṭkōḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ciṟupāṉmai muracaiyum iṅṅaṉam pōkki vaippatuṇṭu. potuvāka, pōrukkup paraṇināḷ ciṟantatākak koḷḷappaṭum.
maṟavar kuṟipārttal vāyppuḷ, uṉṉam, puḷ, viricci eṉa nālvakaippaṭum. vāyppuḷ eṉpatu, taṟceyalākap piṟar vāyiṉiṉṟu kēṭkappeṟum maṅkalac col allatu naṟcol. uṉṉam eṉpatu, oru kuṟippiṭṭa uṉṉamaram taḻaittuvariṉ tam aracaṉukku veṟṟi yeṉṟum, atu paṭṭuppōyiṉ avaṉukkut tōlvi yeṉṟum paṭai maṟavar kuṟittukkoḷḷutal. puḷ eṉpatu, āntai kūkai mutaliya paṟavaikaḷ olikkun taramum kākam vallūṟu mutaliya paṟavaikaḷ paṟakkun ticaiyumpaṟṟik kuṟittukkoḷḷum nimittam. viricci yeṉpatu, pōrmaṟavar pōyt taṅki yirukkumiṭattil naḷḷiravil ōrttukkēṭkum vāṉaccol.
aracar tam makappēṟṟuk kālattil potuvāyp pōrukkuc celvatillai.
pōrukkuc celvataṟku iraṇṭoru nāṭku muṉpu, vaḷḷuvaṉ yāṉaimītēṟi muracaṟaintu ataippaṟṟit talai nakarattārkku aṟivippāṉ. pōrukkup puṟappaṭumuṉ aracaṉ paṭaimaṟavarai ūkkuvittaṟ poruṭṭu avarkku oru ciṟanta virun taḷippatu vaḻakkam. atu peruñcōṟṟunilai eṉappaṭum. ataṉ piṉ pōr maṟavarkku aṭaiyāḷappūvum pōrppūvūm paṭaik kalaṅkaḷum aracaṉāl vaḻaṅkappeṟum. paṭait talaivarkkum, ciṟanta maṟavarkkum iyaṟkaip pūviṟkup patilākap poṟpū aḷikkappeṟuvatumuṇṭu.
aracaṉ tāṉum pōrukkuc celvatumuṇṭu; cellāmaiyu muṇṭu; celvatē perumpāṉmai, aracaṉ taṉakku veṟṟiyeṉṟu muḻuvuṟutiyāyiruppiṉ, pakaivar nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟumuṉṉarē, atait taṉ iravalarkkum paṭait talaivarkkum paṅkiṭṭuk koṭuttuviṭuvatum orōviṭat tuṇṭu. atu koḷḷār tēeṅ kuṟitta koṟṟam eṉappaṭum. (tol. 1013.)
pōrukkup puṟappaṭum aṉṟu pōrmuracukaḷ muḻukkap peṟum. pōrmaṟavar, kuḷittu vaṭṭuṭaiyaṇintu paṭaikkalan tāṅkip pūccūṭi aṇivakuttu, koṟṟavaikku narapaliyiṭṭu, neṭu moḻiyum vañciṉamuṅ kūṟi yāravārittu, nakarukku valamākap puṟap paṭṭuc celvar. poṉṉulakap pēṟṟai virumpum cila maṟavar poṟṟakaṭṭai vāyiliṭṭuk koḷvatuṇṭeṉpatu, cintāmaṇi yāl teriya varukiṉṟatu (778). pōrppaṭaikaḷ pakai nāṭṭūṭu cellumpōtu koḷḷaiyaṭittuc celvatu vaḻakkam.
paṇṭaikkālap pōrkkaruvikaḷ, eypaṭai, eṟipaṭai, kuttup
paṭai, veṭṭuppaṭai, aṭipaṭai, taṭupaṭai eṉa aṟu vakaiya. villampu eypaṭai, viṭṭēṟu eṟipaṭai; vēl īṭṭi kuntam mutali yaṉa kuttuppaṭai; vāḷ veṭṭup paṭai; maḻu taṇṭu mutaliyaṉa aṭipaṭai, kavacam kēṭakam mutaliyaṉa taṭupaṭai.
iṉi, erimaruntu kalveṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭiruppatāl, ippōtu vēṭikkaikkāka viṭappaṭum vāṇam1, paḻaṅka kālattil uḻiñaip pōril payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭirukkalām eṉa ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu.
paṭaikaḷ pōrukkuc cellumpōtu, potuvāka, mutalāvatu kālāṭpaṭaiyum, iraṇṭāvatu kutiraip paṭaiyum, mūṉṟāvatu tērp
paṭaiyum, nāṉkāvatu yāṉaippaṭaiyum cellum. ovvoru paṭaiyilum cēṉaik kaṇimakaṉ eṉṉum kaṇiyaṉ iruppāṉ. cēṉai muḻuvatiṟkum potuvāṉ kaṇiyaṉ cēṉaip peruṅkaṇi eṉappaṭu vāṉ. taṉ nāṭṭil naṭakkum pōrāyiṉum, ayalnāṭṭil naṭakkum pōrāyiṉum, aracaṉ cellum pōtellām avaṉuṭaṉ pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliyōrum celvatu  vaḻakkam. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉuṭaṉ vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟa nāṭaka makaḷir nūṟṟiruvarum, kuyiluvar (vāttiyak
kārar) irunūṟṟeṇmarum, nakaivēḻampar nūṟṟuvarum āvar.
talainakarukkut tolaivāṉa iṭattil naṭakkum pōruk kellām pācaṟai amaittu muḷvēli kōlap peṟum. atu vēṉiṟ kālattil amaikkappeṟiṉ vēṉiṟ pācaṟaiyeṉṟum, kuḷir kālattil amaikkappeṟiṉ kūtaṟ pācaṟai yeṉṟum peyar peṟum. maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷil nīṇṭanāṭpōrkaḷ potuvāka nikaḻttap peṟuvatillai. vēṉiṟpōr kārtoṭaṅkumuṉ allatu toṭaṅkiyavuṭaṉ muṭikkappaṭum. entap pōrukkum ittuṇaik kālam eṉṉum yāppuṟavillai. pōrukkuc celluṅ kālam paṭaikaḷiṉ nilaiyaiyum pōrkkaḷattiṉ tolaivaiyum poṟuttatu. orunāṭ pōrumuṇṭu; pallāṇṭup pōrumuṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ īrāṇṭeṭṭu mātamum, mutaṟ kulōt tuṅkaṉ īrāṇṭum vaṭanāṭṭup pōriṟ celaviṭṭaṉar. pācaṟai pāṭivīṭu eṉavumpaṭum.
pōr toṭaṅkumuṉ, pōrkkaḷattaiyaṭutta vūrkaḷiluḷḷa āniraikaḷ, pārppaṉar, peṇṭir, makappeṟātōr, piṇiyāḷar, ciṟuvar, mutiyōr mutaliyōr avviṭattiṉiṉṟakaṉṟu pātu kāva lāṉa iṭattai aṭaiyumpaṭi eccarikkap peṟuvar. pōrkkaḷat tiṟkup paṟantalai eṉṟu peyar.
pōr toṭaṅkumpōtu, yāṉaippaṭai mikunta aracar tam yāṉaikaḷai viṭṭup pakaip paṭaikaḷaiyuḻakkac ceyvar. avvac camayattiṟkēṟpa, caturam, nīḷcaturam, vil, vaḷaiyam, vaṭṭam, arai vaṭṭam, cakkaram, ciṟai,  viripaṟavai mutaliya vevvēṟu vaṭivil cēṉaikaḷ amaikkappaṭum. pōr neṭukalum muracukaḷ muḻaṅkum. pakaiyaracaṉ talaiyaiyēṉum pakaip paṭaittalaivaṉ talaiyai yēṉum koṇṭuvantu kāṭṭiya porunaṉukku, aracaṉ peruṅ koṭai yaḷippaṉ. atu talaimārāyam eṉappaṭum.
potuvāy, kari pari tēr kāl eṉṉum nālvakaip paṭai kaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟum taṉtaṉ iṉattuṭaṉēyē porum. aṅṅaṉamē paṭaimaṟavar paṭaittavar aracar ākiya muttarattārum tattam tarattiṉaruṭaṉēyē poruvar. paṭaiñar muṉpum paṭaittalaivar piṉpum aracar iṟutiyum poruvatu iyalpu. ciṟupāṉmai paṭai  mayakkam ēṟpaṭṭu im muṟai māṟiyum nikaḻum. palanāṭ pōrāyiṉ, pakaltoṟum porutu irāt toṟum pācaṟaiyil taṅkuvar.
pōriṭaiyē, tōṟkum nilaiyilirukkum aracaṉ pōrk kaḷattiṉiṉṟum pācaṟaiyiṉiṉṟum tūtuviṭuppatum, māṟṟaracaṉ atai matittu uṭaṉpaṭikkaik kiṇaṅkuvatum, matiyātu tūtaṉaic ciṟaiyiṭuvatum peṇkōlam pūṇuvittu aṉuppuvatum uṇṭu.
āṭaiyiḻantavaṉ, kuṭumi kulaintavaṉ, āyutamiḻantavaṉ, pācaṟai pukuntavaṉ, tōṟṟōṭupavaṉ mutaliyōrōṭu porutalum; puṇpaṭṭu viḻuntavaṉaik kollutalum; paṭaimaṭamākak karutap paṭṭaṉa.
pōr muṭivu aracaṉuṭpaṭa aṉaivarum porutu maṭital, īraracarum uṭaṉpaṭikkai ceytal, ciṟaipiṭikkappaṭṭa aracaṉ tiṟai koṭuttal, tōṟṟa aracaṉ ōṭip pōtal ākiya nālvakai nilai kaḷuḷ oṉṟāyirukkum.
veṉṟa aracaṉ, vellappaṭṭa aracaṉai naṭparacaṉākki maṇavuṟavu pūṇal, ciṟṟaracaṉākkit tiṟaiyiṭuvittal. perun taṇṭam iṟuvittal, kāṭṭiṟkut turattip patilāḷiyamarttal, māṉakkēṭu ceytum ceyyātum ciṟaiyiṭal, kollutal ākiya aṟuvakaic ceyalkaḷuḷ oṉṟaic ceyvaṉ. piṟkūṟappaṭṭa munnilaikaḷilum, tōṟṟa aracaṉiṉ tēvimār veṉṟa aracaṉiṉ talainakariluḷḷa vēḷam eṉṉum ciṟaikkōṭṭattil ciṟaiyiṭap paṭuvatuṇṭu. karikāl vaḷavaṉum yāṉaikkaṭ cēy māntarañcēra lirumpoṟaiyum pōl ciṟaiyiṉiṉṟu tappit tam urimaiyaiyum nāṭṭaiyum mīḷap peṟṟārumuḷar.
pōr muṭintapiṉ, aracaṉ taṉ maṟavaruḷ kāyappaṭṭavarait tēṟṟalum pōṟṟalum, porutupaṭṭavariṉ makkaṭku naṉkoṭai yaḷittalum vaḻakkam.
veṟṟiyaracaṉ tōṟṟa aracaṉoṭu naṭpuṟavu pūṇāviṭattu, avaṉ talainakaraik koḷḷaiyaṭittal, ataṉai eriyūṭṭutal, aṅkuḷḷa vayalaḻittal mutaliya aḻipput toḻilum; kaṭṭaṭamum matilumiṭittuk kavaṭi vittutal, kaṭimaran taṭital, kāvaṟ poykaiyai yāṉaikaḷai viṭṭuk kalakkal, nāṭu nakar mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ peyarai māṟṟal 2 mutaliya avamāṉat toḻilum ceytu perumaikoḷvatu perumpāṉmai. koḷḷaiyaṭik kappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷuḷ, karuvūlamum virutukaḷum yāṉai kutiraiyum aracaṉaic cērum; maṟṟavai maṟavaraic cērum.
veṉṟa iṭattil, kalveṭṭal, veṟṟittūṇ nāṭṭal, perumalaiyiruppiṉ atil taṉ ilacciṉaiyaip poṟittal, taṉ āṇai aṭippaṭumvarai taṅkiyiruttal mutaliya ceyalkaḷaiyum veṟṟiyaracaṉ mēṟkoḷvatuṇṭu. veṟṟiviḻā, iyalumpō
tellām, veṉṟa aracaṉ talainakar vellappaṭṭa aracaṉ talainakar ākiya īriṭattum koṇṭāṭappeṟum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉaiyiṭattil atu tappātu nikaḻum. pōril nērnta kuṟṟaṅkaṭkuk kaḻuvāyāka, kōyiṟkum maṟaiyōrkkum ciṟanta koṭaikaḷ nikaḻttappeṟum.
pōriṟpaṭṭa paṭaittalaivarkkum ciṟanta maṟavarkkum, avaruṭaiya ūrum pērum pōrum  cīrum poṟikkappaṭṭa kal naṭappeṟum. atu naṭukal eṉṟum paṭṭavaṉ kuṟi eṉṟum aḻaikkap peṟum. naṭukallai nīrppaṭaiceytu ataṟku mālai cūṭṭip paṭaippatum, kōyileṭuttu viḻavayarvatum uṇṭu.
14
aṟamuṅ koṭaiyum
aṟam: aṟaneṟiyiṟ poruḷīṭṭi ap poruḷāl aṟaneṟiyil iṉpan tuykkavēṇṭum eṉpatai uṇarttaṟkē, aṟanūlār immaip poruḷ mūṉṟaiyum aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṉum im muṟaip paṭuttik kūṟiṉar. ovvoruvaṉum aṟaneṟiyil taṉtaṉ toḻilaic ceyvatē, avaṉavaṉ poruḷīṭṭi iṉpantuykkum vaḻiyākum. aracaṉ toḻilāvatu nāṭukāval. pōrviṉaiyum nāṭukāvaluṭ paṭṭatē. ataṉāl akkālattu aracar perumpālum tam viṉaikaḷi lellām aṟattaik kaṭaippiṭittaṉar.
kuṟaḷarum cintarum, araṇmaṉaiyil makaḷir uṟaiyum uvaḷaka meykāvalarāka amarttappeṟṟaṉar. tēvāram pāṭum kuruṭar patiṉaṟuvar, tiruvāmattūrk kōyilil tēvārappāṭa karāka amarttap peṟṟiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉam cila eccap piṟaviyarkku vāḻkkai vacati ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu.
ciṟuvar, meliyār, tūyar mutaliyōraik kollāmaiyum; porunilaiyaṟṟavaṉ, tōṟṟōṭupavaṉ mutaliyōroṭu porā maiyum; aṭaikkalam pukuntavaṉaik kaiviṭāmaiyum pōraṟaṅ kaḷākap perumpālum kaṭaippiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
paṇṭait tamiḻaracar, tam nāṭṭai allatu kuṭikaḷai āḷum toḻilai āṭci eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟiyātu, kāval puravu ōmpal mutaliya coṟkaḷālēyē kuṟikka virumpiṉar. avar kāval pūṇṭa iṭam, kuṭainiḻal eṉṟum aṭiniḻal eṉṟum kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟa kōvalaṉaik koṉṟatāl taṉ kōl kōṭiṟṟeṉṟu kaṇṭa noṭiyē uyir tuṟappāṉāyiṉ, avaṉ aṟavuṇarcci ettuṇai aḷaviṟantatāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum!
koṭai: akkālat taracar, aṟamnōkkiyum kalai vaḷarcci paṟṟiyum ūḻiyap pārāṭṭākavum, viṉaiyūkkaṟ poruṭṭum, palvēṟu koṭai nikaḻtti vantaṉar. avai uṇṭikkoṭai, poṟkoṭai, ūrtikkoṭai, vilaṅkuk koṭai, ciṉṉak koṭai, peyarkkoṭai, nilakkoṭai, makaṟkoṭai, āṭcik koṭai eṉa oṉpāṉ vakaippaṭum.
pārppaṉarkku nāḷtōṟum uṇavaḷikkumpaṭi, āṅkāṅkuk kōyilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum ūṭṭuppuraikaḷilum nitiyum māṉiyamum viṭṭiruntatu, uṇṭik koṭaiyākum. peruñ
cōṟṟutiyañcēralātaṉ pāratap pōrp paṭaikaḷiraṇṭiṟkum patiṉeṭṭu nāḷum peruñcōṟu vaḻaṅkiyatum, uṇṭikkoṭai yiṉ pāṟpaṭum.
pulavarkkaḷitta pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa poṟkācum, pāṇarkkaḷitta poṟṟāmaraippūvum, pāṭiṉiyarkkum viṟaliyark kum aḷitta poṉṉarimālaiyum, āṭal pāṭal araṅkēṟiya pāṇarkkum kaṇikaiyarkkum aḷitta āyirat teṇkaḻañcu poṉṉum, 1 veṟṟi viḻāt toṭarpāka maṟaiyōrkkaḷitta tulāpāram 2 eṉṉum āḷniṟaip poṉṉum iraṇiyakaruppam eṉṉum poṟkuṭamum poṟ koṭaiyākum.
pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliyōrkkaḷitta kutirai yāṉai tēr eṉpavai ūrtikkoṭaiyākum.
maṟaiyōrk kavvappō taḷitta āvāyiram (kōcakasram) vilaṅkukkoṭaiyākum.
avaikkaḷat talaimaip pulavarkkaḷitta kuṭai koṭi civikai mutaliya virutukaḷum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiyavark kaḷitta talaikkōlum, vaṇikat talaivark kaḷitta eṭṭip pūvum, paṭaittalaivark kaḷitta ēṉāti mōtiramum, amaiccark kaḷitta kāvitippūvum ciṉṉakkoṭaiyākum.
uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāḷark kaḷitta vēḷ aracu eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷum, vaṇikar talaivark kaḷitta eṭṭip paṭṭamum, āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiyavark kaḷitta talaikkōṟpaṭṭamum, amaiccarkkaḷitta kāvitip paṭṭamum, paṭaittalaivark kaḷitta ēṉātippaṭṭamum, palavakai araciyalatikārikaṭ kaḷitta araiyaṉ (rāyaṉ) māvaraiyaṉ (mārāyaṉ) pēraraiyaṉ viḻuppēraraiyaṉ 
mūvēnta vēḷāṉ mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum. ciṟṟaracark kaḷitta piḷḷai makkaḷ nāyaṉār eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷum peyark koṭaiyākum. iṉi, aracar tam kuṭippeyarkaḷai amaiccar paṭaittalaivar mutaliyōrkkum, tam ciṟappup peyarkaḷaic ciṟṟaracarkkum paṭṭappeyarākavum peyaraṭaiyākavum iṭuvatu muṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉiṉ paṭaittalaivaṉ villavaṉ kōtai eṉṟum, arimarttaṉa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ mutalamaiccarāṉa māṇikkavācakar teṉṉavaṉ piramarāyaṉ eṉṟum, mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkac cōḻaṉiṉ ciṟṟaracaruḷ oruvaṉ kulōttuṅkac cōḻap pirutikaṅkaṉ eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruntamai kāṇka.
araciyal viṉaiñar, porunar (pōr maṟavar), potu nala vūḻiyar, pulavar, kalaiñar, vaṇikar, maṟaiyōr, aṟaccālai, maṭam, kōyil mutaliyōrkku aḷitta nilam nilakkoṭaiyākum. atu muṟṟūṭṭu, iṟaiyili, iṟainilam, nilaviṟai ākiya nālvakai nilaimaiyilum; nilam ūr nāṭu eṉṉum mūvakaiyaḷavilum koṭukkappaṭṭatu. oruvakai variyu millātatu muṟṟūṭṭu. atu uṟāvarai, kācu koḷḷā viṟaiyili eṉavumpaṭum. araciṟai maṭṭum nīṅkiyatu iṟaiyili. iṟaiyiṟukkum nilam iṟainilam. nilattil varum iṟaiyaimaṭṭum nukarvatu nilaviṟai.
araciyal viṉaiñarkku, akkālattil campaḷam umpaḷam eṉṉum iruvakaiyil vēlaippayaṉ aḷikkappaṭṭatu. nellāka vēṉum kācākavēṉum koṭuppatu campaḷam; nilamākak koṭuppatu umpaḷam. umpaḷam māṉiyam eṉappaṭum. atu nilaiyāṉatu; perumpālum iṟaiyiliyā yiruppatu.
māṉiyavakai: araciyal viṉaiñaruḷ, ūrttalaivaṉukkuk koṭuppatu ampalamāṉiyam; ūrkkaṇakkaṉukkuk koṭuppatu kaṇakkakkāṇi; veṭṭiyāṉukkuk koṭuppatu veṭṭippēṟu.
araciyal viṉaiñaruḷ orucārārāṉa porunaruḷ, paṭaittalai vaṉukkuk koṭuppatu amaranāyakam; pōriṟ paṭṭavaṉ makaṉukku allatu maṉaivikkuk koṭuppatu irattakkāṇi (irattak kāṇikkai, iratta māṉiyam, utirappaṭṭi).
potunalavūḻiyaruḷ, kuṭimakkaṭkuk koṭuppatu  kuṭimakkaḷ māṉiyam; paṟaiyaṭippavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu paṟaittuṭaivai; uvaccaṉukkuk koṭuppatu uvaccakkāṇi; maruttuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu maruttuvap pēṟu; naccu maruttuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu viṭakara (viṣahara) pōkam; ēriyuṭaippai aṭaittavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu ērippaṭṭi, ivaiyellāvaṟṟiṟkum potuppeyar ūḻiyamāṉiyam eṉpatu. ivaṟṟuṭ pala, ūravaiyārāṟ koṭuk kappaṭṭavaiyēṉum, aracavoppam peṟṟavai.
vaṇikaruṭ ciṟanta toṇṭu ceytavaṉukkuk koṭuppatu eṭṭippuravu.
pulavarkkuk koṭuppatu pulavarmuṟṟūṭṭu.
kalaiñaruḷ, naṭṭuvaṉukkuk koṭuppatu naṭṭuva nilaik kāṇi; kaṇiyaṉukkuk koṭuppatu kaṇimuṟṟūṭṭu.
maṟaiyōr eṉṉum pirāmaṇarukkuk koṭuppatu piramatāyam (piramatēyam.) atu tokutippaṭṭavarukkuk koṭup patum taṉippaṭṭavarkkuk koṭuppatum eṉa iruvakai. akarap paṟṟu, akkiracālaippuṟam, akkirakāravāṭai eṉpaṉa tokutip paṭṭavarkkuriyaṉa. pārppaṉarmaṭṭum kuṭiyirukkum iṟaiyili nilam akarappaṟṟu. pārppaṉar perumpāṉmaiyākavum piṟar ciṟupāṉmaiyākavum kuṭiyirukkum iṟaiyilinilam akkirakāra vāṭai, pārppaṉarai uṇpikkum akkiracālaikku viṭappaṭṭa māṉiyam akkiracālaip puṟam. vētavirutti, paṭṭavirutti, attiyayaṉavirutti (attiya yaṉāṅkam), tayittiriyak kiṭaippuṟam, pāratappuṟam mutaliyaṉa taṉip paṭṭavarkkuriyaṉa.
cattiram, aṭiciṟcālai (ūṭṭuppurai, cōṟṟaṭaippu, aṉṉacattiram) mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku viṭum māṉiyam cālaippuṟam (cālāpōkam). aṭiciṟcālaikkumaṭṭum viṭappaṭuvatu  aṭiciṟpuṟam .taṇṇīrppantalukku viṭappaṭuvatu taṇṇīrppaṭṭi.
maṭattiṟku viṭappaṭuvatu maṭappuṟam.
kōyiṟku viṭappaṭuvatu tēvatāṉam allatu tēvatāṉap paṟṟu (tēvatāyam, tirunāmattukkāṇi, tiruviṭaiyāṭṭam, tiraviṭaip paṟṟu.) kuṭiyirukkum makkaḷōṭu cērttuk kōyiṟ kaḷikkappaṭum nilam kuṭi nīṅkāt tiruviṭaiyāṭṭam eṉappaṭum. piṭārikōyiṟku viṭappaṭum māṉiyam piṭāripaṭṭi allatu piṭāriviḷākam eṉṟum, camaṇa pauttap paḷḷikaṭku viṭap paṭuvatu paḷḷiccantam eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
aṟam nōkkiyum matampaṟṟiyum viṭappaṭum māṉiya mellām potuvāka aṟappuṟam eṉappeṟum.
pārppaṉarkkum kōyiṟkum oru muḻuvūrait tāṉañ          ceyyum pōtu, oru peṇyāṉaiyai av vūraic cuṟṟip pōkaviṭṭu, atu pōṉavaḻiyē ellaiyamaittuk kalluṅ kaḷḷiyum naṭṭi, nīraṭṭik koṭuttu, perum paṇaikkāraṉ eṉṉum atikāri vāyilāy, cep pēṭṭil aracāṇai (āṇatti) poṟippatu marapu. peṇyāṉaiyai viṭṭu ellaiyamaittal piṭicūḻntu paṭākai naṭattal eṉappaṭum. tāṉañ ceyyappaṭum ūriluḷḷa uḻuvittuṇpār kārāṇmai eṉṉuñ collā lum, uḻuvittuṇpār mīyāṭci eṉṉuñ col lālum kuṟikkappeṟuvar. iṉi, ōr ūrai mēṟpārkkuṅ kūṭṭamum mīyāḷuṅkaṇam eṉappaṭum. nīraṭṭik koṭutta ceppēṭṭut tāṉa mellām aṭṭippēṟu eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
kōyiṟku viṭappaṭum nilamellām iṟaiyiliyāka irukka vēṇṭi yiruntamaiyiṉ, aracaṉ tāṉāyc ceyyāta tēvatāṉa mellām avaṉuṭaiya icaivu peṟṟē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṅṅaṉañ ceyyumpōtu, iṟainīṅkalāl araciṟku ēṟpaṭum iḻappiṟku īṭāka, tāṉañ ceypavaṉ allatu ceypavar oru kuṟitta tokaiyaip paṇṭārattiṟ cērkkumpaṭi ūravaiyāriṭam koṭuttuviṭuvatu vaḻakkam.
oru nilam allatu ūr iṟaiyiliyākkappaṭum pōtu, ataṉ iṟainīkkam iṟaṅkal eṉṟum, atai iṟaiyilip pativup puttakattil peyartteḻututal nīṅkal allatu nāṭṭu nīṅkal eṉṟum kuṟikkap peṟum. varikkaṇakkaiyum nilaṅkaḷiṉ nilaimai yaiyum entac camayattilum terintu koḷḷaṟ poruṭṭu, palvēṟu kaṇakkukaḷ vaikkappaṭṭiruntamaiyiṉ, cila camayaṅ kaḷil ōr iṟaiyili āṇaikku aimpatiṉmarukku mēṟpaṭṭa atikārikaḷ kainnāṭṭiṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. iṟutiyil aracaṉ iṭum muṭivāṉa āṇai kaṭaiyīṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
nāṭṭuk kuḻappattiṉāl iḻakkappaṭṭa māṉiya vurimaiyai, kuḻappam nīṅkiyapiṉ cāṉṟukāṭṭi mīṇṭum peṟṟukkoḷḷa akkālattu iyalkai (cāttiyam) iruntatu. kaḷappāḷar āṭciyil iḻakkappaṭṭa vēḷvikkuṭip piramatēya vurimai, iru nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṉ mīḷa nāṭṭappeṟṟa pāṇṭiya vāṭciyiṉ ēḻām talaimuṟaiyil putuppikkappeṟṟatu.
cēramāṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēlkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ, paraṇarkkut taṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēralaik koṭuttatu makaṟkoṭaiyākum. makaṟ koṭaiyāvatu makaṉai māṇavaṉākak koṭuttal.
takaṭūreṟinta peruñcēra lirumpoṟai, aricilkiḻārkkut taṉ ariyaṇaiyaik koṭuttatu āṭcikkoṭaiyākum. āyiṉ, avar atai ēṟka maṟuttu avaṉiṭam amaiccup pūṇṭār.
koṭaimaṭam: paṇṭaiyaracaruṭ palarkkuk koṭai oru ciṟanta kuṇamāyiruntapōtē, oruvakaiyiṟ kuṟṟamākavum iruntatu. atu koṭaimaṭam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pēkaṉ ōr aruviṉai yuyarvilaik kaliṅkattai oru mayilmēṟ pōrttāṉ. pāri taṉ iḻaiyaṇi neṭuntērai oru mullaikkoṭi paṭara niṟuttiyatoṭu, taṉakkirunta munnūṟūr kaḷaiyum paricilarkkīntu viṭṭāṉ. imayavarampaṉ neṭuñcēra lātaṉ, taṉmēṟ pattuppāṭṭup pāṭiya kumaṭṭūrk kaṇṇa ṉārkku, ainnūṟūr piramatāyamum muppatteṭṭiyāṇṭu teṉṉāṭṭuḷ varuvataṉiṟ pākamuṅ koṭuttāṉ. kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic caral, kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉārkku, pattuppāṭṭiṟku nāṟpatilakkam poṉṉum tāṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamuṅ koṭuttāṉ. iṅṅaṉam, aḵṟiṇai yuyirikaṭkut takāta poruḷkaḷaiyum, aracu keṭumpaṭi paricilarkku nāṭu muḻuvatai yum, pala pulavar paciyāl vāṭa ōriru pulavarkku māperuñ celvat taiyum aḷittatu koṭaimaṭamām.
15
kuṟṟamun taṇṭaṉaiyum
kuṟṟa vakai: kuṟṟaṅkaḷ, aṟanūlukku māṟāṉavum, araciyalukku māṟāṉavum, teyvattiṟku māṟāṉavum eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
kuṭi poy kaḷavu kāmam kolai eṉpaṉa aṟanūlukku māṟāṉavai. ivai aimperuṅ kuṟṟam eṉappaṭum. piṟariṭam vāṅkiṉatai maṟuttalum, taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa piṟar poruḷai oḷittalum, kaḷḷak kaiyeḻuttum, āḷmāṟāṭṭamum poyyuḷ aṭaṅkum.
iṟaiyiṟukkāmai, uṟuporuḷ kavartal, pakaiyoṟṟu, aṟai
pōtal, aracaṉatu poruḷ kavartal, aracaṉatu iṉpap poruḷ nukartal, ūrukku allatu nāṭṭiṟku iraṇṭakañ ceytal, aracaṟ
kiraṇṭakañ ceytal, aracāṇai mīṟal, koḷḷaiyaṭittal, kalakañ
ceytal eṉpaṉa araciyaṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷām. putaiyalum piṟaṅkaṭaiyillāc cottum uṟuporuḷākum.
oppip paṇiceyyāmai, kōyiṟ poruṭkavarvu eṉpaṉa teyvattiṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷ. kōyilil viḷakkerippatait taṇṭaṉaiyākap peṟṟavaṉum, kōyilil viḷakkerikkap poṉ peṟṟavaṉum, aṅṅaṉam erikkāmaiyum; kōyiṟ paṇṭārattiṟ peṟṟa kaṭaṉukku vaṭṭiyiṟukkāmaiyum; oppip paṇiceyyāmai yākum. kōyiṟ kalaṅkaḷaiyum aṇikalaṅkaḷaiyum kaḷavāṭalum, vaḻipāṭṭu viḻāc celavaik kuṟaittalum aṭiyōṭu niṟuttalum kōyiṟ poruṭ kavarvākum.
mūvakaik kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷḷum, kaṭumaiyāṉavai mēṟpaṭu kuṟṟam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
taṇṭaṉai vakai: taṇṭaṉaiyāṉatu, taṇṭam, māṉakkēṭu, vētaṉaippāṭu, ciṟaippu, vēlainīkkam, kolai eṉa aṟuvakaip paṭṭiruntatu.
avaṟṟuḷ, taṇṭam eṉpatu tokaiyiṟuppu, taṇṭat tīrvai, kōyil viḷakkerippu eṉa mūvakai. tokaiyiruppāvatu oru kuṟitta tokaiyai mottamāyc celuttal. iṉi, maṉṟu
pāṭu taṇṭā kuṟṟam eṉa mūvakait taṇṭamuṅ kūṟappaṭum. avai iṉṉaveṉṟu tiṭṭamāyt teriyavillai.1 māṉakkēṭu eṉpatu, mukattiṟ cempuḷḷi karumpuḷḷi kuttik kaḻutaimē lēṟṟi āṉmayan tōytta alakālaṭittu ūrvalam varuvittilum, kulattiṉiṉṟu vilakkalum ākum.
vētaṉaippāṭu eṉpatu, tūṇiṟ kaṭṭivaittu 50 aṭi mutal 100 aṭivarai aṭittal, kallēṟṟal, veyilil niṟpittal, kiṭṭi pūṭṭal, naṭaiviḷakkerittal eṉap palavakaippaṭum. kiṭṭi eṉpatu keṇṭaikkālai nerukkum oruvakaik karuvi. naṭai viḷakkerittal eṉpatu, talaiyil akalviḷakkēṟṟi ūrvalam varuvittal.
ciṟaippu eṉpatu, talaiyiṭṭuc ciṟaik kōṭṭattil vaittal, kolai eṉpatu, veṭṭal, kaḻuvēṟṟal, vilaṅkāṟ kolvittal, cittiravatai ceytal eṉa nālvakai. yāṉaiyai viṭṭu mitippittalum pulikkaruttutalum pōlvaṉa vilaṅkāṟ kolvittal. erumaik kāliṟ kaṭṭiyōṭṭutalum vaṇṭic cakkarattiṟ kaṭṭiyōṭṭutalum pōlvaṉa cittiravataiyākum.
kuṟṟat taṇṭaṉai: mūvakaik kuṟṟavāḷiyarkkum kuṭa vōlaiyiṭappeṟāmai potut taṇṭaṉaiyākum. kaṭkuṭikku itu tavira vēṟu taṇṭaṉaiyillai.
aṟanūlukku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ, poykkut taṇṭamum; kaḷaviṟkup poruḷ mīṭpoṭu māṉakkēṭum, poruḷmīṭkap peṟā
viṭattut taṇṭamum, taṇṭam iṟukkappaṭāviṭattuc ciṟaiyum; kāmattiṟku māṉakkēṭum kolaikkuk kolaiyum; taṇṭaṉai yākum.
kolai ceytavaṉ ōṭippōṉaviṭattu, avaṉ cottu muḻutum paṟimutalākik kōyiṟkuc cērkkappaṭṭatu. ōṭippōṉa kolaiñaṉaik kolvataṟkup potumakkaṭkum urimaiyirun tatu. kolaiñaṉ kolaiyuṇṭapiṉ, avaṉatu paṟimutalāṉa cottaip piṟaṅkaṭaiyāṉ vēṇṭip peṟṟukkoḷḷa muṭiyum. taṟceyalāy nērnta kolaikkuk kōyil viḷakkerippē taṇṭaṉaiyāka vitikkappaṭṭatu. kolaiyāṉatu virumpic 
ceyyappaṭṭaviṭattum, kolaiñaṉ makaṉ kolaiyuṇṭavaṉiṉ neruṅkiyavuṟaviṉaroṭu oppantañ ceytu koḷḷiṉ, taṇṭaṉai nīṅka iṭamiruntatu.
narapali, maṟpōr, koṭumpāvi kaṭṭiyiḻuttal ākiya mūviṭattum nikaḻuṅ kolaikkut taṇṭaṉaiyēyillai. taṟkolaiyait taṭuppataṟkuc caṭṭamuṟaiyāṉa taṭaiyum akkālat tillai.
araciyaṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ, iṟaiyiṟukkāmaikkuc cottup paṟimutalum, uṟuporuḷ kavartalukkuc ciṟaiyum,1  piṟavaṟṟiṟkuk kolaiyum 2 potuvāka vitikkappaṭum taṇṭaṉai yākum. iṟaiyiṟātārkku īrāṇṭut tavaṇaiyum, ataṉmēṟ kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkup piṇaippēṟum tarappaṭṭaṉa. ataṉ piṉṉum iṟātāṉ cottu paṟimutalākik kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, araciṟait tokaipōka eñciyatellām kōyiloṭu cērkkappaṭṭatu. koḷḷaiyaṭittavar kalakañ ceytavar poruḷkaḷum paṟimutal ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
teyvattiṟku māṟāṉa kuṟṟaṅkaṭkut taṇṭamum, kuṟṟavāḷi kōyil viṉaiñaṉāyiṉ ciṟupāṉmai vēlai nīkkamum taṇṭaṉaiyāyiruntaṉa. kōyil viṉaiñaruḷ, kīḻōr kuṟṟattai mēlōrum; mēlōr kuṟṟattai ūravaiyār, nāṭṭatikāri, araca ṉāl vitantu viṭukkappaṭṭa atikāri, aracaṉ ākiyavaruḷ oruvar allatu palarum kēṭṭut tīrppatu vaḻakkam. oppip paṇi ceyyātavarkkuk kaṭuntaṇṭavariyum kōyiṟporuḷ kavarnta pūcakaṉ aṟaikāraṉ (ukkirāṇattāṉ) mutaliyōrkkut taṇṭamum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. aṇikalamum taṭṭumuṭṭuṅ kaḷavāṭiyavarāl iṟukkappaṭṭa taṇṭat tokaiyaik koṇṭu, ap poruḷkaḷ vāṅki vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. taṇṭamiṟukka iyalāta pūcakar tam kōyiṉ muṟaināḷkaḷil cila pala viṭṭuk koṭuttaṉar.
perumpālum kuṟṟaṅkaṇṭē taṇṭaṉai vitikkappaṭṭatu. aiyuṟavāṉaviṭattil makkaḷai vataittuk kēṭpatum uṇṭeṉ patu, paḻiyañciya tiruviḷaiyāṭalāl aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. akkālattil naṭuvunilaiyāka muṟai vaḻaṅkappaṭṭateṉpatu, maṉumuṟaic cōḻaṉ poṟkaip pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi 
mutaliyōr ceytiyāl aṟiyalām. āyiṉum, cilaviṭattu ārāyā mal taṇṭaṉai vitikkap paṭṭateṉpatu, vārttikaṉ, kōvalaṉ, paṭṭiṉattār mutaliyōr varalāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum, paṭṭiṉat tār tam niṟaimoḻi vaṉmaiyālēyē taṉṭaṉaiyiṉiṉṟu tappiṉar.
vali kuṉṟiya aracar kālattil aracarkkaṭaṅkātu vāḻnta makkaḷum uḷar. avar kūṭivāḻnta ūr aṭaṅkāppaṟṟu eṉappaṭṭatu.
16
ulavuṅ kaittoḻilum
uḻavaṉ uyarvu: uṇaviṉṟi uyirvāḻkkai yiṉmaiyiṉ, av vuṇavai viḷaikkum uḻavar maṉṉuyirt tērukku accāṇi yākavum, maṉpatai marattiṟku āṇivērākavum karutappaṭṭaṉar.
``uḻuvār ulakattiṟ kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟā
 teḻuvārai yellām poṟuttu''	(kuṟaḷ. )
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
ciṟappāka irappārkkoṉ ṟīpavarum viruntiṉaraip pēṇu pavarum uḻavarēyāvar.
``iravā rirappārkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
 kaiceytūṇ mālai yavar''
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvarum ``irappōr cuṟṟam'' eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭi kaḷum,
``vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpāṉ viruntirukka vuṇṇātāṉ''  (tirikaṭu.12)
eṉṟu nallātaṉārum,
``ennāḷum - kāppārē vēḷāḷar kāṇ''
eṉṟu kamparum kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
viruntōmpalum irappōrk kītalumākiya vēḷāṇmai ceyvatiṉālēyē uḻavar vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. vēḷāṇmai yāvatu piṟarai virumpip pēṇutalai  yāḷun taṉmai. vēḷ - viruppam.
uḻavar amaitik kālattil uḻavuttoḻilaic ceytuvanta tōṭu, pōrkkālattil pōrppaṇiyum purintu vantaṉar.
``vēntuviṭu toḻiliṉ paṭaiyuṅ kaṇṇiyum
vāyntaṉar eṉpa avarpeṟum poruḷē''	(1582)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka
antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum nāṟperuṅ kulattāruḷ, vēḷāḷar ēṉai mukkulat tillaṟattāraiyum tāṅkivantatiṉāl, vēḷāḷarē ciṟanta illaṟattārākak karutappaṭ ṭaṉar.1 maruta nilattūril nilaiyāka vacittu āṟiloru kaṭamaiyai aracaṉukku oḻuṅkāka iṟuttuvantavarum vēḷāḷarē.
vaṇṇāṉ, mayirviṉaiñaṉ, cemmāṉ, kuyavaṉ, kottaṉ, kollaṉ, kaṉṉāṉ, taṭṭāṉ, taccaṉ, kaṟṟaccaṉ, cekkāṉ, kaikkōḷaṉ, pūkkāraṉ, kiṇaiyaṉ (kiṇaippaṟaiyaṉ), pāṇaṉ, kūttaṉ, vaḷḷuvaṉ, maruttuvaṉ ākiya patiṉeṇ toḻilārum; uḻavaṉukkup pakkattuṇaiyā yiruntatu tattam toḻilaicceytu  avaṉiṭam kūli allatu tām ceyta poruṭku vilai peṟṟu vantaṉar. itaṉāl, avar patiṉeṇ kuṭimakkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭu vēḷāḷaruḷ aṭakkappaṭṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam palvakuppāraiyum uṇavaḷittuk kāttatiṉā lēyē, vēḷāṇviṉaiyait tirukkaivaḻakkam eṉac ciṟappittuk kūṟiṉār kampar. teyvattiṟkup paṭaitta tiruccōṟṟaip palarkkum vaḻaṅkuvatu tirukkai vaḻakkam eṉappaṭum.
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ
  kaimpulattā ṟōmpal talai			(kuṟaḷ 43)
eṉṟu ilvāḻkkai yatikārattum,
iruntōmpi yilvāḻva tellām viruntōmpi 
 vēḷāṇmai ceytaṟ poruṭṭu		      (81)
vittu miṭalvēṇṭuṅ kollō viruntōmpi
 miccil micaivāṉ pulam			      (85)
eṉṟu virumtōmpalatikārattum
uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvārmaṟ ṟellān
 toḻutuṇṭu piṉcel pavar			    (1033)
eṉṟu uḻavatikārattum, vaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruppatāl, uḻavaṉē talaimaik kuṭivāṇaṉ (chief citizen) eṉṟu avar koṇṭamai pulaṉākum.
makkaḷ vāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta ūṇuṭaik karuvip poruḷkaḷum, araciyaṟ kiṉṟiyamaiyāta iṟaiyum, pōrukku nēr 
vakaiyum nēralvakaiyumāṉa paṇiyum uḻavarāl amaivatai nōkkumpōtu,
palakuṭai nīḻalun taṅkuṭaikkīḻk kāṇpar
 alakuṭai nīḻa lavar		(1034)
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvarum,
porupaṭai tarūuṅ koṟṟamum uḻupaṭai
 yūṉṟucāl maruṅki ṉīṉṟataṉ payaṉē
 ................................................
 pakaṭupuṟan tarunar pāra mōmpik
 kuṭipuṟan tarukuvai yāyiṉiṉ 
 aṭipuṟan tarukuvar aṭaṅkā tōrē	(puṟam. 35)
eṉṟu veḷḷaikkuṭinākaṉārum,
purappōr koṟṟamum - uḻaviṭai viḷaippōr
(cilap. 10 : 149-50)
eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, kūṟiyiruppatu oru ciṟitum mikai yākātu.
iruvakai vēḷāḷar: vēḷāḷar, uḻutuṇpārum uḻuvit tuṇpārum eṉa iruvakaiyar. uḻutuṇpārukkuk karuṅkaḷamar kārāḷar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum, uḻuvittuṇpārukku veṇ kaḷamar veḷḷāḷar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum uriyaṉa. uḻavar, kaḷamar, kaṭaiyar, vēḷāḷar eṉpaṉa irucārārukkum potuvākum. āyiṉum, īṟṟup peyar tavira ēṉaiyavellām uḻutuṇpārkkē ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkiṉa. avarukku maḷḷar eṉṉum peyaruṇṭu. avar tannilattil uḻuvārum piṟar nilattil uḻuvārum eṉa iru nilaimaiyar.
uḻavittuṇpār palar vēḷ eṉavum aracu eṉavum paṭṭameyti, amaiccarum paṭaittalaivarum maṇṭalat talai varum ciṟṟaracarumāki, mūvēntarkkum makaṭkoṭai nērum takuti yarāyiruntaṉar. kaṭai yeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷuṭ perumpālār vēḷirē.
nilavakai: nilaṅkaḷ iṉṟiruppatu pōṉṟē, naṉcey puṉcey vāṉāvari (vāṉāṅkāṇi) eṉa mūvakaippaṭṭiruntaṉa. uḻutu payiriṭappaṭuvatu uḻavukkāṭu eṉṟum, kottip payiriṭap paṭuvatu kottuk kāṭu eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
ceyaṟkai nīrvaḷam: uḻavuttoḻiṟku iyaṟkai nīrvaḷam pōtāviṭattu, aracarāl ceyaṟkai nīrvaḷam amaikkappaṭṭatu. veḷḷac cētam nērāvāṟum, pāyccalukku vēṇṭiya nīr ōṭu māṟum, āṟṟiṟkuk karai kaṭṭalum; nīrait tēkka vēṇṭumiṭattil āṟṟiṟkuk kuṟukkē aṇai kaṭṭalum; pērāṟṟiṉiṉṟu kaṇṇā ṟum, kaṇṇāṟṟiṉiṉṟu kālvāyum, kālvāyiṉiṉṟu vāyk kālum veṭṭalum; ivai iyalāviṭattu, ēri kuḷam toṭutalum akkālattaracar mēṟkoṇṭa ceyalkaḷām. kaṇṇāṟuṅ kāl vāyum perumpālum cōḻanāṭṭilum, ēri kuḷam perumpālum pāṇṭi nāṭṭilum veṭṭap paṭṭaṉa.
karikāl vaḷavaṉ īḻattiṉiṉṟu paṉṉīrāyiram makkaḷaic ciṟai piṭittuk koṇarntu kāvirikkuk karai kaṭṭuvittāṉ. irācēntirac cōḻaṉ cōḻakaṅkam eṉṉum ēriyai veṭṭiṉāṉ. muṭikoṇṭāṉ āṟum avaṉāl veṭṭappaṭṭatu pōlum! kāvirik kallaṇai ki.pi. 1068l vīrarācēntiraṉāl veṭṭap paṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
cōḻanāṭṭil iṉṉiṉṉa kaṇṇāṟu iṉṉiṉṉa vaḷa
nāṭṭiṟkum, iṉṉaṉiṉṉa kālvāy iṉṉiṉṉa vūrukkum, iṉṉiṉṉa vāykkāl iṉṉiṉṉa pāṭaka varicaikkum, pāyavēṇṭumeṉṉum ēṟpāṭiruntatu. ataṉāl vēliyāyiram viḷaiyavum, orupiṭi paṭiyuñ cīṟiṭam eḻukaḷiṟu purakkavum, irupūvum muppūvum eṭukkappaṭavum iyalvatāyiṟṟu. pūveṉpatu veḷḷāṇmai.
aracariṉ ūkkuvippu: nilam cariyāy viḷaiyātaviṭattum kaṭun taṇṭalāḷaraippaṟṟi muṟaiyiṭṭaviṭattum, aracar varinīkkañ ceytaṉar. ayalnāṭukaḷiluḷḷa ariya viḷaiporuḷ kaḷaik koṇṭuvantum tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ payiriṭac ceytaṉar.

tarumpeṟa lamiḻta maṉṉa
karumpivaṭ ṭantōṉ perumpiṟaṅ kaṭaiyē	(puṟam. 392)
eṉṟu atiyamāṉ makaṉ pokuṭṭeḻiṉiyai auvaiyār pāṭi yiruppatāl, avaṉ muṉṉōruḷ oruvaṉ cīṉat tiliruntō cāvakattiliruntō, karumpait tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.2 marutanilat teyvamākiya vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉukkup pukārc cōḻar viḻāk koṇṭāṭiyatum, avaṉ uḻavut toḻiṟku vēṇṭum maḻaivaḷan taruvaṉ eṉṉum kuṟikkōḷ paṟṟiyatē pōlum!
17
vaṇikamum pōkkuvarattum
uḻaviṉāl viḷaikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷaiyum kaittoḻi lāṟ ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷaiyum uḷnāṭṭiṟ pakiravum, uḷ nāṭṭup poruḷkaḷai veḷināṭṭilum veḷināṭṭup poruḷkaḷai uḷ nāṭṭilum parappavum, perum poruḷīṭṭi nāṭṭuc celvattaip perukkavum, vaṇikam iṉṟiyamaiyāta teṉṟu kaṇṭa paṇṭaiyaracar, ataṉai ollum vāyellām ūkkivantaṉar.
nakarantoṟum vaṇikar kuḻumam (Merchant Guild) iruntatu. nāṭṭuppuṟa nakarāṭci perumpālum nakara vaṇikar kaiyil iruntatu. nakaraṅkaḷaiyellām aracaṉ nēraṭiyāka mēṟpārttu vantaṉaṉ. talaimai vaṇikaṉukku eṭṭip paṭṭamum eṭṭippuravum aḷikkap paṭṭamai muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. vaṇikar kuḻumaṅkaṭku valañcai maṇikkirāmam añcuvaṇṇam mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē, nilavāṇikam nīrvāṇikam ākiya iruvakai vāṇikamum tamiḻakattil taḻaittōṅki yiruntaṉa. nilaṅkaṭantu ceyyum nilavāṇikam kāliṟpirivu eṉṟum, nīrkaṭantu ceyyum nīr vāṇikam kalattiṟ pirivu eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūuvō ṭillai	(980)
eṉṉun tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē nīrvāṇikam ciṟantiruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
nilappōkkuvarattu: taṭivaḻi yeṉṟum peruvaḻi yeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum cālaikaḷ nāṭeṅkum iruntaṉa. avai 64 cāṇ akalamum irumaruṅkum maraṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉavāyiruntaṉa. kōṭ ṭāṟṟuc cālai, maturaip peruvaḻi araṅkam nōkkip pōnta peruvaḻi koṅkuvaḻi taṭikaivaḻi, vaṭukavaḻi mutaliya peyarkaḷāl, tamiḻnāṭṭup perunakarkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟit teṉṉāṭṭuc cīmaikaḷum cālaikaḷāl iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntamai pulaṉām. toṇṭaināṭṭiṉ mēṟkellai oru peru vaḻiyāyirunta teṉpatu, mēṟkup peruvaḻiyām eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum. āṅkāṅkuḷḷa ūravaiyār avaravar ūrarukē yuḷḷa taṭivaḻip pakutikaḷaik kavaṉittu vantaṉar.
vēṭṭaikkucceṉṟaaracaṉneṭumāṉ ṟēroṭupākaṉainilavumaṇaṟkāṉyāṟṟuniṟkappaṇittu........ïU«bghÊšpukum”ednakkīra®kūṟiyiruppatāl,iyaṉwviṭamellā«caṅkakkāla¤taraca®tērēṟi¢ceṉṟaṉareṉṟum,ataṟkuriavaḻivacatikaŸamaikka¥paṭṭiruttašvēṇṭumeṉṟu«ūkikkalām.
cālaikaḷ piriyumiṭaṅkaḷil allatu cērumiṭaṅkaḷil, avvac cālai cellum ūrp peyar poṟitta vaḻikāṭṭi maraṅkaḷ nāṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
māṭu, kutirai, kōvēṟukaḻutai ākiya mūvakaip poti mākkaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṇikaccāttukaḷ, aṭikkaṭi cālaikaḷil iyaṅkikkoṇṭiruntaṉa.
kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum kauntiyaṭikaḷum aruvilaic cilampuṭaṉ pukāriliruntu muppatu kātat tolaiviluḷḷa maturaikkum, tēvantiyumkaṇṇakiyi‹ceviliyu«aṭittōḻiyu«pukārilirunJmatuuvaḻiyāŒvañcikkum,maṇimēkalapukāriliruªtuvañcikFmpiṉdrvañciyiliruªtukāñcikku«,māṭayṉpukāriṉi‹ṟukumarikFmpiṉṉa®kkaṅkaikku«,parācauṉcōṇāṭṭiṉi‹ṟurranāṭṭiṟFmpi‹pupeUmporuḷulṉpāṇṭinā£ṭuvaḻiyāfttaṉṉūrukku«,yātēhriṭarppahṭumiṉ¿cceṉṟatāš,akkālatâlvaḻippōkkar¡kuvēṇLmvaḻivacatiímpātukāpòmnira«paviruntaimayaṟiyappaṭu«.
tamiḻaracar aṭikkaṭi vaṭanāṭṭiṉmēṟ paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟatum; avaruḷ cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭṭuc celavil, 100 tērkaḷum 500 yāṉaikaḷum 10,000 kutiraikaḷum, 20,000 paṇṭa mēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaḷum, 1,000 caṭṭaiyiṭṭa atikārikaḷum, māperun tokaiyiṉarāṉa kālāṭ paṭaiñarum, 102 nāṭaka makaḷirum, 208 kuyiluvarum, 100 nakai vēḻamparum uṭaṉ ceṉṟa tum; akkālattirunta pōkkuvarattu vacatiyai oruvāṟuṇarttum.
aṉuppu eṉṟoru vari muṟkālattu vāṅkappaṭṭatiṉāl, orukāl añcal ēṟpāṭu akkālat tiruntirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
nīrp pōkkuvarattu: paḵṟuḷiyāṟu muḻuku muṉṉarē, kiḻakkilcāvakam malaiyā kāḻakam (parmā) cīṉam mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷuṭaṉum, mēṟkil arapiyā pāpilōṉiyā kaltēyā mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷuṭaṉum, tamiḻakam vaṇikañ ceytu vantatu.
cīṉakkaṇṇāṭi cīṉakkāram cīṉakkiṇṇam cīṉaccūṭaṉ cīṉappaṭṭu mutaliyapaṇṭaṅkaḷcīṉattiṉiṉṟumilavaṅkappaṭṭai,kirāmpū,cātikkāy,cātippattirimutaliyacarakkukaḷnākanāṭukaḷeṉṉumkīḻintiyat ÔvukTṭṭattiṉiṉṟum,1jmiḻaracariṉelvakaipgṭaikaḷuḷxṉṟāṉaFtiraippaṭaikkunvaṇṭuṅFtiraikaḷmrapiyāviṉiṉṟum,áttirappēḻaigvaiviḷakkuktuKtaliyabpāruḷkaḷavaṉaeṭukaḷiṉiṉṟumjmiḻakattiṟkukflaṅkaḷiltntiṟaṅkiṉa.nj¡F, tōkai(mayil),arici,akil,cantaṉam,iñci,koṭṭai(pañcuccuruḷ),veṟṟilai,aṭaikkāy(pākku)mutaliyaglabpāruḷkaṭkutjmiḻpbpayarkaḷēnmalaibmāḻifḷiltḻaṅkuvatu,gḻantamiḻeṭṭuVṟṟumaticáṟappaikfṭṭum.fšnjah ehṭṭaicrrntaū®e‹ṉumilttilafḻnjṭukkappaṭṭaoUtaÄḻnāṭṭutjkkavu¤taram,ki.K.3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 1000 āṇṭu kaṭku muṟpaṭṭa cālōmōṉ eṉṉum yūtavaracaṉ kālattil tamiḻakattiṉiṉṟu yūtēyāviṟku ēṟṟumatiyāṉa tōkaiyiṉ peyar yūta moḻiyāṉa epirēyattil tuki eṉa vaḻaṅki vantatu.
kaṭalvāṇikattaip perukkutaṟporuṭṭu, ki.mu. 55-l pāṇṭiyaṉ  rōmavaracaṉukkum, ki.pi. 1015-l mutalām irācarācaṉum,   ki.pi. 1015-l irācēntiraṉum ki.pi. 1077- l mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉum cīṉavaracaṉukkum tūtu viṭuttaṉar.
muttamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkaraiyilum, oru kālattiṟku oṉṟum palavumāka, vevvēṟu kālattil vevvēṟu tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ amaintiruntaṉa. cōḻanāṭṭiṟku mayilai, mallai, pukār, kāraikkāl, nākai, toṇṭi mutaliyaṉavum; pāṇṭināṭṭiṟkuk kavāṭam, koṟkai, kāyal mutaliyaṉavum; cēranāṭṭiṟku vañci, muciṟi, toṇṭi, māntai, naṟavūr, koṭuṅkōḷūr, kāntaḷūr, viḻiñam, kōḻikkōṭu2 mutaliyaṉavum tuṟainakarkaḷāy iruntuvantaṉa.
tuṟaimukantōṟum kalaṅkarai viḷakkam (Light House) iruntatu. pukār kavāṭam vañci pōṉṟa talaimai allatu kōnakart tuṟaimukaṅkaḷil, palvēṟu nāṭṭuk kalaṅkaḷ palvakaip paṇṭaṅkaḷai nāḷtōṟum ēṟṟuvatum, iṟakkuvatumā yiruntaṉa. ēṟṟumatiyum iṟakkumatiyumāṉa aḷaviṭappaṭāta paṇṭap potikaḷ. āyattiṟkāka niṟukkappaṭṭu avvat tamiḻnāṭṭaraca muttirai poṟikkappaṭṭapiṉ, tuṟaimukattaiviṭṭu nīṅkumvarai ciṟanta kāvalceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṇiyavum cēyavumāṉa palvēṟu nāṭṭiliruntu vanta pulampeyar mākkaḷ taṅkuvataṟku, vacatiyāṉa cērikaḷum viṭutikaḷum iruntaṉa. tamiḻakak kīḻkaṭaṟ karaiyil nākanāṭṭār kumari mutal vaṅkamvarai pala nakaraṅkaḷil vantu taṅkiyiruntamaiyāl, avai nākarkōyil, nākappaṭṭiṉam, nākūr, nākapuri eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. mīṉ piṭikkum kaṭṭumaram mutal kutiraip paṭaiyēṟṟat takka nāvāyvarai palatarappaṭṭa marakkalaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṇikarkkuc contamāyiruntaṉa. ēlēlaciṅkaṉ, kōvalaṉ muṉṉōr, paṭṭiṉattār eṉṉum tiruveṇkāṭar mutaliyōr peruṅkala vaṇikarāvar. ``ēlēla ciṅkaṉ kappal ēḻukaṭal ceṉṟālum mīḷum'' eṉpatu paḻamoḻi yākum.
cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ ``kaṭaṟkaṭam peṟinta kāvalaṉ'' eṉṟum, ``kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ'' eṉṟum pukaḻap peṟuvatāl akkālattaracar pakaivarālum kaṭaṟkoḷḷaik kārarālum viḷaikkap paṭum tīṅkukaḷaip pōkkik kaṭal vaṇikattaik kāttamai ūkikkappaṭum. pīlivaḷai nākanāṭṭi ṉiṉṟu pukār vantu mīḷavum, maṇimēkalai īḻattiṟkum cāvakattiṟ kum ceṉṟu mīḷavum, vacatiyum pātukāvalumāṉa marakkalap pōkku varattu akkālattiruntatu.
nīriṉ vanta nimirparip puraviyuṅ
kāliṉ vanta karuṅkaṟi mūṭaiyum
vaṭamalaip piṟanta maṇiyum poṉṉuṅ
kuṭamalaip piṟanta vāramu makilun
teṉkaṭal muttuṅ kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟukiruṅ
kaṅkai vāriyuṅ kāvirip payaṉu
mīḻat tuṇavuṅ kāḻakat tākkamu
mariyavum periyavu neriya vīṇṭi	(185-192)
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉappālaip pakutiyāl, pukārukku vantu cērnta iruvakai vaṇikap poruṭperukkai oruvāṟuṇaralām.
18
kalaiyuṅ kalviyum
paṇṭait tamiḻakattil kalvi ikkālattiṟpōl araciyal tiṇaikkaḷaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāka iruntatillai. āciriyariṉ muyaṟci yiṉālum potumakḷiṉ pōṟṟaraviṉālumē, potukkalvi nāṭṭiṟ parappappeṟṟu vantatu. kaṟṟuvalla pulavaraiyē aracar ōraḷavu pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
potukkalvi: eḻuttum eṇṇum kaṟpitta tuvakkak kalviyāciriyarkku iḷampālāciriyar eṉṟum, ciṟṟilakkaṇamum nikaṇṭum kaṇakkuṅ kaṟpitta naṭuttarak kalviyāciriyarkkuk kaṇakkāyar eṉṟum, aintilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkuriya ilakkiya muṅ kaṟpitta mēṟṟarak kalviyāciriyarkku āciriyar eṉṟum peyar. āciriyar, nūlāciriyar nuvalāciriyar (pōtakāciriyar) uraiyāciriyar eṉa mūvakaiyar.
tuvakkakkalvi māṇavarkkum naṭuttarak kalvi māṇavarkkum, paḷḷippiḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟum, māṇiyar eṉṟum māṇavar (māṇavakar, māṇākkar) eṉṟum, caṭṭar eṉṟum peyar. avaruḷ talaimai yāṉavaṉ caṭṭanampi allatu caṭṭanampip piḷḷai (caṭṭāmpiḷḷai) eṉappaṭṭāṉ. mēṟṟarak kalvi māṇavarkku māṇavar eṉṟum maḻapulavar eṉṟum, kaṟṟuccolliyar eṉṟum peyar.
tuvakkakkalvi nilaiyilum naṭuttarakkalvi nilaiyilum māṇavariṉ peṟṟōrum, mēṟṟarak kalvi nilaiyil māṇavarum, āciriyarkkuc campaḷam iṟuttu avaraip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. ēḻaiyāyiruntu āciriyaṉukkut toṇṭu ceyyum māṇavaṉukku muttarak kalviyum ilavacamāyk kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu.
ciṟappuk kalvi: māṇavar potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, icai nāṭakam kaṇiyam maruttuvam mutaliya ciṟappuk kalaikaḷai, avvak kalai yāciriyar vāyilāyk kaṟṟaṉar. akkālattu nūlkaḷellām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviliruntamaiyiṉ evvakai nūṟkalvikkum ciṟanta ilakkaṇak kalvi iṉṟiyamaiyātatāyiruntatu.
poruḷ koṭuppōrkkum paṇiviṭai ceyvōrkkum kalaip payiṟci yaḷikkappaṭṭatu.
meynnūṟ kalvi: potukkalvi kaṟṟapiṉ, allatu potukkalviyuñ ciṟappukkalviyuṅ kaṟṟapiṉ, meypporuḷaṟivu peṟa virumpiṉavar, kāṭṭakattuḷḷa muṉivariṭam allatu yōkiyariṭam ceṉṟu mūvāṇṭu meynnūṟ kalvi kaṟṟatākat terikiṉṟatu.
vēṇṭiya kalvi yāṇṭumūṉ ṟiṟavātu	(1134)
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam.
ceyyuḷiyaṟṟum tiṟam: eṇṇum eḻuttum tavira evvakaik kalviyum ceyyuḷ vāyilākavē kaṟṟamaiyālum, iḷamaiyi liruntu ceyyuḷ ceytum ceyyuḷiṟ pēciyum payiṉṟamaiyālum, paṇṭaip pulavarellām ceyyuḷiyaṟṟun tiṟam ciṟakkap peṟṟiruntaṉar.
aracar tamiḻai vaḷarttalum pulavaraip pōṟṟalum: pāṇṭiyar toṉṟutoṭṭuk ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai kaḻakam (caṅkam) irutti, nūlnilaiyam pōṟṟi, 1 mutunūlārāycciyum putunūlākkamum nikaḻvittu, muttamiḻai vaḷarttu vantaṉar. kaḻakaṅ kulaintapiṉ, muttamiḻaracarum tattam avaiyil ōriru ciṟanta pulavarai yamarttip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. avaikkaḷap pulavarkkum, nūlaraṅkēṟiyavarkkum, aracaraip pukaḻntu pāṭiyavarkkum muṟṟūṭṭum ciṟanta paricum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭal pāṭal vallārum araṅkēṟi āyiratteṇ kaḻañcupoṉ peṟṟamai muṉṉarē kūṟappaṭṭatu. araṅkēṟṟam aracaṉ muṉṉilaiyilum avvat tamiḻiṟ ciṟanta atikāriyiṉ talaimaiyilum naṭaipeṟ ṟatu. araṅkēṟiya nūlāciriyarai veṇpaṭṭaṇivittu yāṉaimē lēṟṟi nakarvalam varuvippatu vaḻakkam.
ilakkiyap pulavarum camayanūṟ pulavarum coṟpōr nikaḻtti uṇmai nāṭṭaṟkut, talainakartoṟum paṭṭimaṇṭapam eṉṉum tarukkamaṇṭapam iruntatu. pulavariṭait teyvappulamaiyaiyum piṟaruḷḷak karuttaṟiyum āṟṟalaiyum vaḷarttaṟporuṭṭu, aracar avvappōtu tam uḷḷattiluḷḷa kiḷarccimikka karuttaippaṟṟi avaraip pāṭaccolvatuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam pāṭappaṭuvatu kaṇṭa citti eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaruṭaiya uḷḷakkaruttai aṟivippatum aiyuṟavait teḷivippatumāṉa pāṭaṟkup poṟkiḻiyaḷikkappaṭṭatu. 
ittakaiya camaiyaṅkaḷil, iṅṅaṉam pāṭiṉārkkitu veṉṟu viḷampi muṉṉarē poṟkiḻiyaic caṅkamaṇṭapattil tūkki viṭuvatu pāṇṭiyar vaḻakkam.
kuṟikkai (camaciyai) pāṭalum, arikaṇṭam emakaṇṭam pōṉṟa kaṭumpā (ācukavi) pāṭalum, aracar avvappōtu nallicaip pulavarkkum kaṭum pāvalarkkum naṭatti vanta ceyyuṭ pōṭṭi vakaikaḷākum.
takka pulavar talaimaiyamaiccarākavum tūtarākavum amarttap peṟṟaṉar. avaikkaḷat talaimaip pulavarkkum, araṅkēṟiya kuṭaikoṭi mutaliya koṟṟac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. pulavariṉ parinturaikkum vēṇṭukōṭkum iṇaṅkuvatum, avaratu aṟivuraiyaiyum aṟavuraiyaiyum iṭitturaiyaiyum ēṟṟuk koḷvatum, avaratu pulanturaiyaiyum paḻippuraiyaiyum poṟuttuk koḷvatum, cevviyaṟiyātum viṭaipeṟātum eccamayattilum ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuḷ avaraip pukaviṭuvatum, avarukkup paṇiviṭai ceyvatum, avarai vaḻiviṭukkumpōtu ēḻaṭi piṉpōy mīḷvatum, akkālat taracar palar ceyalkaḷākum.
paricaḷiyāviṭattum tammai avamatittapōtum pulavar aracaraic cāvippatumuṇṭu.
kalvittara vuyarvu: akkālap pulavar kaṭuttup pāṭum āṟṟaluṭaiyarāyiruntu ceyyuḷilēyē tammuḷ uraiyāṭi vantaṉar. nikaṇṭu eṉṉum akarātiyuṭpaṭa aṉaittilakkiyamum ceyyuḷ vaṭivilēyē iruntaṉa. maturaic caṅkattilum piṟa pulavaravaiyilum araṅkēṟiya piṉṉarē nūlkaḷ nāṭṭil ulavalāyiṉa.
kalvi vaḷarcci: talaiccaṅkattil irunta pulavar ainnūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaraip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar eḻuvareṉṟum; iṭaiccaṅkattil irunta pulavar aimpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟuvar eṉṟum, avaraip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar aivar eṉṟum; kaṭaic caṅkattil irunta pulavar  nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar eṉṟum, avaruḷḷiṭṭup pāṭiyavar nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉ mar eṉṟum, avaraip pōṟṟiya pāṇṭiyaruḷ kaviyaraṅkēṟiyavar mūvar eṉṟum muccaṅka varalāṟu kūṟum.
itupōtuḷḷa kaṭaiccaṅka ilakkiyattiliruntu aṟiyap paṭum pulavar ēṟattāḻa ēḻunūṟṟuvar āvar. avaruḷ makaḷir irupat tiruvarum aracar paṉṉiruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarum āvar.
aṟuvaivāṇikaṉ, ōlaikkaṭaikāraṉ, kaṇiyaṉ, kūttaṉ, kūlavāṇikaṉ, pāṇaṉ, porunaṉ, maruttuvaṉ, vaṇṇakkaṉ mutaliya palvakait toḻilāḷarum; kāvaṟ peṇṭu, araci, kuṟamakaḷ, pēymakaḷ mutaliya palatara makaḷirum pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
kaṭaic caṅkattiṟkup piṟkālattilum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉārum kulacēkarāḻvārum kaṇṭarātittarum ativīrarāma pāṇṭiyaṉum pōlum aracarum, tiruveṇkāṭarpōlum vaṇikarum, 2ām auvaiyārum kāraikkālammaiyārum pōlum makaḷirum palar pulavarāyiruntaṉar.
varalāṟṟiṟ keṭṭāta pulavar, caṅkakālattilum ataṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum ettuṇaiyarō aṟiyōm.
ācukavikaḷ kācu eṉṟu piṟkālattil vāṅkappaṭṭa vari, aracavoppam peṟṟu avar vāḻkkaic celaviṟku vāṅkap paṭṭatu pōlum!
aracar ilakkiyat toṇṭu: aracar pulavarai nūliyaṟṟu māṟu ūkkiyum tāmum nūliyaṟṟiyum vantatuṭaṉ, pala taṉip pāṭalkaḷaiyum pāvum poruḷum aḷavum paṟṟit tokuttum tokuppittum uḷḷaṉar, naṟṟiṇai tokuppittōṉ pāṇ ṭiyaṉ paṉṉāṭu tanta māṟaṉvaḻuti; kuṟuntokai tokuttōṉ pūrikkō; aiṅkuṟunūṟu tokuppittōṉ yāṉaikkaṭcēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟai; akanānūṟu tokuppittōṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirapperuvaḻuti.
19
camayamum koḷkaiyum
immai maṟumai nalaṅkaṭkum vīṭṭiṉpattiṟkum iṟai vaṉaruḷ iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉṉuṅ koḷkaipaṟṟi paṇṭait tamiḻaracar tamakkum tam kuṭikaṭkum nalam varuvittaṟporuṭṭut tamakkicaintatoru camayattaik kaṭaippiṭittu iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.
tamiḻaracaruṅ kuṭikaḷum mutalāvatu aintiṇait teyvaṅ kaḷai vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. kuṟiñcikkuc cēyōṉ eṉṉum murukaṉum, mullaikku māyōṉ eṉṉun tirumālum, marutattiṟku vēntaṉ eṉṉum intiraṉum, neytaṟku varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) eṉṉum kaṭalōṉum, pālaikku māyōḷ eṉṉuṅ kāḷi (koṟṟavai)yum, teyvamāyiruntaṉar.
varuṇa vaṇakkam nāḷaṭaivil niṉṟuviṭṭatu. intira vaṇakkam kaṭaiccaṅkat tiṟutivaraiyiruntatu. maṟṟa mutteyva vaṇakkamum neṭukalun toṭarntatu.
kaṭaiccaṅka kālattil, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilum maturai yilum vañciyilum paṟpala teyvaṅkaṭkuk kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. pukārilirunta kōyilkaḷ kaṟpakak kōyil, veḷyāṉaik kōyil, palatēvar kōyil, katiravaṉ kōyil, kayilaikkōyil, murukaṉ kōyil, vaccirappaṭaikkōyil, cāttaṉkōyil, arukaṉ kōyil, nilāk kōyil, civaṉkōyil, tirumāl kōyil mutayaṉa. murukaṉ, civaṉ, tirumāl, palatēvaṉ ākiya nālvarkkum ellāt talainakarkaḷilum kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa.
teyvaṅkaḷ perunteyvam eṉṟum ciṟuteyvam eṉṟum iruvakaippaṭum. camayat teyvaṅkaḷ perunteyvamum maṟṟavai ciṟuteyvamumākum. caivam vaiṇavam ārukatam pauttam piramam ulakāyatam eṉṉum āṟum kaṭaiccaṅka kālattu aṟu camayam eṉṉalām. akam akappuṟam puṟam puṟappuṟam eṉa nālāṟu camayamākap pakuttuk kūṟiyatu piṟkālattatākum, civavaḻipāṭum mālvaḻipāṭum toṉṟutoṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭiṟkuriyavātaliṉ av viraṇṭum paṟṟiya caivamum vaiṇavamum tamiḻaracarāl perumpālum kaṭaip piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.   av viraṇṭaṉuḷḷum civaneṟiyē aracariṭaic ciṟantiruntatu. iṟaivaṉ eṉṟum piṟavā yākkaip periyōṉ eṉṟum civaṉukku vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷē, civaneṟit talaimaiyai uṇartti viṭum.
ciṟuteyvaṅkaḷ, kāṟṟum neruppum mutaliya iyaṟkait teyvamum cūraramakaḷum pūtamum mutaliya pēytteyvamum, katiravaṉun tiṅkaḷum mutaliya kōḷteyvamum, paṭṭa maṟavaṉākiya naṭukalteyvamum, iṟanta kaṟpiyākiya pattiṉitteyvamum, nālvakai varuṇattiṟkuriya varuṇatteyvamum, āṟum, malaiyum nakarum mutaliya iṭattiṟkuriya iṭatteyvamum, kalviyum kātalum pōrum mutaliya toḻiṟkuriya toḻiṟṟeyvamum, kāppumaṭṭum aḷippatākak karutappaṭum kāpput teyvamum eṉap palavakaiyām.
kaṭaiccaṅka kālamvarai, aracarum pulavarum potumakkaḷu mākiya muttiṟattāriṭaiyum, camaviyaṟ koḷkaiyum piṟar matap poṟaiyum iruntuvantaṉa. ataṉpiṉ camayap pōr tamiḻnāṭṭil talai tūkkiṟṟu. mutalāvatu, vaitika camayam eṉappaṭum caiva vaiṇavaṅ kaṭkum avaitika camayam eṉappaṭum camaṇa pauttaṅ kaṭkum iṭaiyē pōr eḻuntatu. piṉṉarc camaṇapauttaṅkaḷ oṭukkappaṭṭapiṉ, caiva vaiṇavaṅkaṭkiṭaiyē pōr mūṇṭatu.
caṅkakkālat taracarum mutalām irācarācaṉ irācēntiraṉ 
mutaliya piṟkālat taracarum, tām kaṭaippiṭitta camayak kōyil kaṭku maṭṭumaṉṟip piṟa matakkōyilkaṭkum māṉiyam viṭṭaṉar. āyiṉ 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum camaṇaṉumāṉa makēntiravarmap pallavaṉ caiva camaya kuravaruḷ oruvarāṉa tirunāvukkaracaraip palavakaiyil vataittuk kolla muyaṉṟāṉ. atē nūṟṟāṇṭiṉa ṉum civaṉaṭiyāruḷ oruvaṉumāṉa neṭumāṟaṉ eṇṇāyiram camaṇaraik kaḻuvēṟṟiṉāṉ. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉaṉum caivaṉu māṉa 2ām kulōttuṅkaṉ, kūrat tāḻvāṉ periyanampi eṉṉum iru vaiṇava kuravarai muṟaiyē kaṇ paṟittuc cākaṭittāṉ. atōṭu tillai maṉṟilirunta tirumāluru vattaiyum peyarttuk kaṭalil eṟintuviṭṭāṉ. aṅṅaṉam orucila aracar neṟi tiṟampi naṭantaṉar. tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār nākaiyilirunta poṟ putta vuruvaik koḷḷaiyaṭittut tiruvaraṅkak kōyilaip putuppit tāreṉṟu, oru paravai vaḻakkuc ceyti vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
20
tiruppaṇikaḷ
immai maṟumai vīṭeṉṉum mummai yiṉpattiṟkum iṟaivaḻi pāṭum iṉṟiyamaiyātatākak karutappaṭṭamaiyiṉ paṇṭait tamiḻakattil iṅkilāntiṟpōl matattuṟaiyum ōr araciyal tiṇaik kaḷamāka iruntuvantatu. piṟa matak kōyilkaṭkum iṭaiyiṭai māṉiyam viṭappaṭṭatēṉum, caiva vaiṇavak kōyilkaḷē perumpālum aracariṉ āṭcikkuṭ paṭṭiruntaṉa.
pēraracarellārum oṉṟum palavumākap peruṅkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭi vaittaṉar. ki.pi. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻaṉ eḻupatu civaṉ kōyilkaḷaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ki. pi. 10ām 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta mutalām irācarācaṉ tañcaip peruvuṭaiyār kōyilaik kaṭṭi vilaiyēṟappeṟṟa aṇikalaṅ kaḷ ūrtikaḷ taṭṭu muṭṭukaḷuṭaṉ 35 ūrkaḷait tēvatāṉamāka viṭṭāṉ. kōyil kaṭṭāta aracar iṟaiyiliyum aṇikalaṅkaḷum piṟavum aḷittaṉar.
kōyiṟku kōṭṭam, kuṭicai, tēvakulam, taḷi, nakaram, paḷḷi, mantiram, māḷikai, ampalam mutaliya pala peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. kōyilkaḷ ciṟukōyil peruṅkōyil eṉa irutiṟattaṉa. ciṟu kōyilkaḷai ūravaiyārum peruṅkōyilkaḷait taṉik kuḻuvārum kavaṉittu vantaṉar. peruṅkōyilai mēṟpārp pavarkkuk kōyil vāriyattār eṉṟum srīkāriyam pārppār eṉṟum, avaruḷ talaivaṉukku srīkāriyak kaṇkāṇi nāyakam eṉṟum peyar. civaṉ kōyilai mēṟpārppavar makēcuvarar eṉṟum, tirumāl kōyilai mēṟpārppavar mākavaiṇavar allatu tāṉattār eṉṟum potuvākak kūṟap peṟuvar. kōyiṟ kāriyaṅkaḷai naṭattivaippataṟku āṉavāḷ eṉṟōr araciyal viṉaiṉum iruntāṉ.
kōyiṟ pūcārikkut tiruvaṭi piṭippāṉ eṉṟum, pōṟṟi eṉṟum, nampiṉāṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ tuṇaivaṉukku eṭuttuk kainīṭṭi eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu.1 ivar aṇukkat toṇṭarāvar. ivarallātu tirukkaik kōṭṭi yōtuvār, tēvaraṭiyār (patiyilār), cākkaiyaṉ, paṇṭāri, aṟaikāraṉ, tēvakaṉmikaḷ, meḻukkaṭikaḷ, tiruviḷakkiṭuvār, tirumeykkāppu (tirumeykāp pōṉ, tirumēṉi kāval) mutaliya ettuṇaiyō viṉaiñar kōyilaic cērntiruntaṉar. perunāṭu muḻutumirunta tirukkaik kōṭṭi yōtuvārkkut tēvāra nāyakam eṉṟoru talaiva ṉiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
iṟaiyili (tēvatāṉam) , naṉkoṭai, nērttikkaṭaṉ, kāṇikkai, vari, taṇṭam, paṟimutal ākiya eḻuvakaiyil kōyiṟku varumāṉam vantukoṇṭiruntatu.
kōyiṟ celaviṟkellām potuppaṭa viṭappaṭṭirunta iṟaiyiliyuṭaṉ, vevvēṟu celaviṟkut taṉittaṉi avvappōtu aracarālum perumakkaḷālum viṭappaṭṭu vanta iṟaiyilikaḷum uḷa. avai tiruviḻāppuṟam, kaṟṟaḷippuṟam, nantavaṉappuṟam, aṭukkaḷaippuṟam, avalamutuppuṟam, akkāravaṭici lamutuppuṟam, meḻukkaṭippuṟam, tiruvaṉantaṟ kaṭṭaḷai mutaliyaṉa. kōyiṟ paṇiyāḷarkkup pakirappaṭum tiruccōṟṟiṟkāka viṭappaṭum kaṭṭaḷai aṭṭiṟpēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. oruvar ciṟitukālam nukarntapiṉ kōyiṟku viṭumāṟu aḷikkappaṭum nilattiṟku iṟaik kaṭṭaḷai eṉṟu peyar.
kōyilil viḷakkēṟṟuvataṟkum piṟa tiruppaṇikkaṭkum pārppaṉarai uṇpippataṟkum, vēṟu vēṟāka, aracarālum peru makkaḷālum, potumakkaḷālum kōyilil nitikaḷum tokai kaḷum oppaṭaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ vaṭṭiyaik koṇṭē ap paṇikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭu vantamaiyiṉ, am mutalkaḷ aḻiyā tiruntaṉa. vaṭṭi policai eṉappaṭṭatu. tām kuṟitta tiruppaṇi kaḷum aṟaṅkaḷum nāḷtōṟum allatu nilaiyāka naṭaipeṟaṟ poruṭṭu, avaṟṟaip policaiyaik koṇṭē naṭattavēṇṭu meṉṟu, koṭaiyāḷarē kuṟittuviṭuvatu marapu.
paṟimutalāṉa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷiṉ cottukaḷ kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, araciṟai niluvaipōka eñciyatellām, kōyiloṭu cē rkkappaṭṭatu.
kōyil varumāṉattil celavupōka eccamellām aṟamuṅ
kalviyumākiya potunalap paṇikkuc celaviṭappaṭṭatu. 
akkālattuk kōyilkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu pala tuṟaiyil potunalat toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉa.
(1) toḻiṟ ṟuṟai: kōyil nilaṅkaḷ ēḻai yuḻavariṭattum, kōyiṟ kaṉṟukālikaḷ ēḻai yiṭaiyariṭattum, vārac cākupaṭik kum vaḷarppiṟkum viṭappaṭṭaṉa.
cuntarapāṇṭiyaṉ kālattil, cūralūrai yaṭuttirunta kōyiṟ kuḷamum vāykkālum aṇaiyum, piḷḷaiyāṉ eṉṉum cempaṭavaṉ pārvaiyilum, avaṟṟiṉ paḻutupārppu cūralūr veṭṭiyāṉiṭattum, viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. piḷḷaiyāṉukkuk kōyiṟkuṭikaḷ celuttum vāykkāl pāṭṭamum pācip
pāṭṭamum, veṭṭiyāṉukkuc campaḷamum umpaḷamum, kaim
māṟāka aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.2 kaṇkāṇippu, vaḻipāṭu, maṭait
toḻil, tuppuravākkam, alaṅkarippu, kaṇakku, kāval mutaliya paltuṟaipaṟṟiya aluvalāḷa rōṭu, icaivāṇarum kūttarum kammiyarum paṇimakkaḷum kuṭimakkaḷu māka ettuṇaiyō pēr kōyilil nilaiyāka amarttappeṟṟut takka campaḷam peṟṟu vantaṉar.
tañcaip peruvuṭaiyār kōyilil, tēvāra ōtuvār nāṟpatteṇmarum patiyilār nāṉūṟṟuvarum, icaikkaruvi
yiyakkuvār paṟpalarum, cākkaiyarum, kaṇiyarum, aivakaik kollarum, kuyavarum, taiyaṟkārarum, vaṇṇārum, muṭiyalaṅkāra viṉaiñarum uṭpaṭa ēṟattāḻa āyiravar nilaiyāṉa viṉaiñarāka amarttap peṟṟiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. pañcakālattum piṟa kālattum, uṇaviṟku vaḻiyaṟṟa āṭavarum peṇṭirum kōyiṟ kaṭimai pukkuk kōyiṟpaṇiyāṟ piḻaittuvantaṉar. avarukku maṇakkavum kuṭumpa vāḻkkai naṭattavum urimaiyiruntatu.
(2) paṇattuṟai: paṇam vēṇṭiyavarkku, vaṭṭik kīṭākak kōyilil kuṟippiṭṭa viḷakkēṟṟumāṟu, kōyiṟ paṇṭārat tiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
varakuṇapāṇṭiyaṉāl, tiruccentiṟ kōyil nitta vaḻipāṭṭiṟkāka 1400  poṟkācu paṉṉīrūrāriṭaip pakirntu   koṭukkappaṭṭatu. vaṭṭi āṇṭiṟku oru kāciṟku irukalam nel eṉṟum, vaṭṭiyaik koṇṭu vaḻipāṭṭai naṭappikkavēṇṭu meṉṟum, vaṭṭi niluvaiyāyiṉ iraṭṭiyum 25 kācu taṇṭamum iṟukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum vitikkappaṭṭaṉa. 3
aṟattiṟkum kaṭṭaḷaikkum kōyiṟkuk koṭukkappaṭum paṇat tokaikaḷ, kōyilatikārikaḷiṭamāvatu, ūravaiyāriṭa māvatu, nakarattāriṭamāvatu, taṉippaṭṭavariṭamāvatu oppaṭaik kappaṭuvatuṇṭu.
(3) kalvittuṟai: eṇṇāyiram eṉṟa iṭattil oru maṟai nūl ( vēta) viṭutik kallūriyum, tirumukkūṭal eṉṉum iṭattil oru vaṭamoḻi viṭutik kallūriyum tiruvoṟṟiyūril viyākaraṇa tāṉa maṇṭapam eṉṉum vaṭamoḻip pāṇiṉiyilakkaṇak kallūriyum, kōyilōṭu cērkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. eṇṇāyirattil 500 māṇavarum 10 āciriyarum iruntaṉar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil vāṉanūlum tarukkamum pirapākaram eṉṉum mīmāmcai nūlum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. māṇavarkkuk kalviyum ūṇuṭaiyuṟaiyuḷum ilavacamāy aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkup pōtiya iṟaiyilikaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
pala iṭaṅkaḷil itikāca purāṇaṅkaḷ potumakkaṭkut tamiḻil viritturaikkappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟkum iṟaiyili viṭappaṭṭi runtatu.
(4) kalaittuṟai: icai kūttu ōviyam ciṟpam vaṇṇam mutaliya pala kalaikaḷ, kōyil vāyilāy vaḷarkkappeṟṟaṉa.
(5) varalāṟṟut tuṟai: kalveṭṭukaḷ, kōyil,maṇṭapam, pāṟai, veṟṟittūṇ, malaikkukai, paṭimai, naṭukal mutaliya palaviṭaṅkaḷil veṭṭappaṭṭaṉavēṉum, perumpāṉmaiyākak kōyilkaḷilēyē avai iṭampeṟṟaṉa.
ki.pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa tamiḻaracar varalāṟṟai 
aṟitaṟku uṟutuṇaiyāṉavai kalveṭṭukaḷē. aracaruṭaiya pōrc  ceyalkaḷaiyum veṟṟiyaiyuṅ kūṟum. meykkīrttikaḷum, ūravai yārukkum nāṭṭatikārikaṭkum piṟarkkum viṭukkappaṭṭa aracāṇai kaḷum, ūravaiyārāl aṉṟaṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa putut tīrmāṉaṅkaḷum, aracarālum atikārikaḷālum perumakkaḷālum koṭukkappaṭṭa tāṉap paṭṭayaṅkaḷum kallil veṭṭappaṭṭuk kōyilil ellāruṅ kāṇu miṭattiṟ patikkappeṟuvatu, tavirā vaḻakkamāyiruntatu.
oru citainta kōyilaip putuppikkumpōtu, ūravaiyār nāṭṭatikāri vāyilāy aracaṉukkaṟivittu avaṉatu icaivu peṟṟa piṉ, kōyililuḷḷa kalveṭṭukaḷai ``icciri vimāṉamiṭittuk kaṭṭuṅkāl kaṇṭa cilālēkaippaṭi'' eṉṟu toṭaṅki ōlaip paṭi yeṭuttu, putuk kalveṭṭup poṟittu, kōyil kaṭṭi muṭintapiṉ, ap putuk kalveṭṭukaḷai atikārikaḷ kāṭṭu miṭattil patippatu vaḻakkam. kalveṭṭup paṭiyeṭuppu kaṟpaṭi māṟṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. citaintupōṉa kalveṭṭukaḷaip putup pikkumpōtum immuṟaiyē kaiyāḷap peṟum.
(6) aṟattuṟai: pārppaṉarai yuṇpikkum akkira
cālaiyum, aṭiyārai yuṇpikkum maṭamum, irappōrai yuṇpikkum aṭiciṟ cālaiyum ākiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ oṉṟō palavō, pala vūrkaḷiṟ kōyilō ṭiṇaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. perum
paṟṟappuliyūril irunta aṟaccālai aṟapperuñ celvi eṉṟum, tirumuk kūṭalil cāttirar eṉṉum pārppaṉa vaṭamoḻi māṇavarkku maruttakamākavum maruttuvak kallūriyākavu mirunta āyurvēta maruttuvacālai (ātulacālai) `vīracōḻaṉ' eṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa.
(7) araciyaṟ ṟuṟai: araciṟai yiṟukkāta nilaṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum kōyiṟku viṟkappaṭṭu, av vilait tokaiyiṉiṉṟu avaṟṟiṟkuriya araciṟai yiṟukkappaṭṭatu,
ūr maṇṭapamillāta vūrkaḷilellām, araciyaṟ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kōyil maṇṭapattilēyē kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. 
cila aracar kōyiṟpaṇiyoṭu tirunūṟpaṇiyum ceytu vantaṉar. mutalām irācarācaṉ tirumuṟai kaṇṭatum, mūṉṟām kulōttuṅkaṉ periya purāṇam iyaṟṟuvittatum nūṟpaṇiyām.
kōyiṟ kaṇakkukaḷ āṇṭutōṟum nāṭṭatikārikaḷāl taṇikkai ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷ taṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar.
21
aracar viḻākkaḷ
aracar viḻākkaḷ potuvum ciṟappum eṉa irupāṟpaṭum. avaṟ ṟuṭ potuvāvaṉa :
(1) maṇṇumaṅkalam: aracaṉ mutaṉ muṟaiyāka muṭicūṭum pōtum, āṇṭutoṟum varum muṭicūṭiya nāḷilum, koṇṭāṭap peṟum viḻā maṇṇumaṅkalam ākum. aḵtu nīrāṭi muṭicūṭum maṅkala viṉaiyātalāl maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉappaṭṭatu. maṇṇutal - nīrāṭutal. tolkāppiyar itaṉaic
ciṟanta cīrtti maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉpar 	(1037)
uḻiñaip pōril, matiṟkaṇ ōraracaṉ maṟṟō raracaṉaik koṉṟu avaṉ kuṭikkalam mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu, paṭṭa vēntaṉ peyarāl muṭipuṉaintu nīrāṭum maṅkalam,
`kuṭumi koṇṭa maṇṇumaṅkalam' 	(tol.1014)
eṉappaṭum. atu akattōṉ maṇṇumaṅkalamum puṟattōṉ maṇṇumaṅkalamum eṉa iruvakai.
māṟṟaracaṉ matilaiyaḻittuk kaḻutaiyērāl uḻutu veḷḷai varakuṅ koḷḷuṅ kavaṭiyum vitti, amaṅkalamāyiṉa ceyta veṟṟivēntaṉ avaṟṟiṟkuk kaḻuvāyāka nīrāṭum maṅkalam, 
`maṉṉeyil aḻitta maṇṇu maṅkalam'		(tol. 1037) 
eṉappaṭum.
(2) perumaṅkalam: āṇṭutoṟuṅ koṇṭāṭap peṟum aracaṉ piṟantanāṭ koṇṭāṭṭam perumaṅkalam ākum. atu perunāḷ eṉavum paṭum. atil aracaṉ uyirkaḷiṭattuk kāṭṭum aruṭkaṟikuṟiyākat tūya veḷḷaṇi yaṇintu, ciṟaippaṭṭavarai viṭutalai ceytu, kolaiyuñ ceruvum oḻintu, iṟaitavirtalum tāṉañceytalum piṟavum mēṟkoḷvatu vaḻakkam.
(3) veṟṟi viḻā: aracaṉ pōril peṟṟa veṟṟiyait taṉṉakarilāyiṉum, māṟṟāṉ nakarilāyiṉum, īriṭattumāyiṉum, koṇṭāṭuvatu veṟṟiviḻāvākum. itaiyoṭṭi, ampalam poṉvēytal tiruvītiyamaittal mutaliya tiruppaṇikaḷum, tulāpāram iraṇiya karuppam mutaliya tāṉaṅkaḷum ceyvatu vaḻakkam.
(4) makappēṟṟu viḻā: aracaṉukkup piḷḷai piṟantapōtu koṇṭāṭappaṭum viḻā makappēṟṟu viḻāvākum. peṇ makap pēṟṟiṉum āṇ makappēṟum, āṇ makappēṟṟiṉum paṭṭattiṟ kuriya mutaṉ makaṟpēṟum ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭappeṟum.
(5) araṅkēṟṟu viḻā: muttamiḻum ōriru tamiḻumpaṟṟi nūliyaṟṟiya āciriyar,araṅkēṟṟiyataik koṇṭāṭum viḻā araṅkēṟṟu viḻāvākum.
(6) naṭukal viḻā: pōril paṭṭa ciṟanta maṟavarkkuk kalnaṭum viḻā naṭukalviḻā vākum.
(7) pattiṉi viḻā: ciṟanta pattiṉip peṇṇukku, avaḷ iṟantapiṉ kal allatu cilai nāṭṭum viḻā pattiṉi viḻāvākum.
iṉi, ciṟappāvaṉa : paḵṟuḷi neṭiyōṉ eṭutta munnīr viḻāvum, mucukuntaṉum neṭumuṭikkiḷḷivaraippaṭṭa avaṉ vaḻiyiṉarum koṇṭāṭiya intira viḻāvum mutalām irācarācaṉ piṟanta nāḷiṟ koṇṭāṭappaṭṭa catayat tiruviḻāvum pōṉṟavai ciṟappu viḻāvākum.
22
cila araciyaṟ karuttukaḷ
paḻantamiḻaracarum pulavarum potumakkaḷum, piṉ varumāṟu pala araciyaṟ karuttukaḷaik koṇṭiruntaṉar:
 (1)	aracaṉ ulakattiṟku uyir.
 (2)	teyvam,  tāṉ, taṉ viṉaiñar, kaḷvar, pakaivar, vilaṅku 	ākiya aṟuvaḻiyil vanta tīṅkukaḷai aracaṉ nīkkit taṉ 	kuṭikaḷaik    kāttal vēṇṭum.
 (3)	aracaṉ toḻilkaḷ, ōtal vēṭṭal ītal kāttal taṇṭittal eṉa  aintu.
 (4)	aracaveṟṟikku aṟaneṟiyāṭci kāraṇam.
 (5)	aracaveṟṟikku uḻavuttoḻil kāraṇam.
 (6) 	ceṅkōlaracaṉ nāṭṭil maḻaivaḷam poḻiyum; makkaḷ oḻukkan tappār. vilaṅku ūṟu ceyyātu; kalviyum aṟamun ḻaikkum. koṭuṅkōl nāṭṭil ivai nikaḻā.
 (7)	koṭuṅkōl aracaṉ āḷum nāṭṭiṉum, kaṭumpuli vāḻuṅ kāṭu naṉṟu.
 (8)	kōṉnilai tiriyiṉ kōḷnilai tiriyum.
 (9) 	aracaṉ iṟappaik kōḷnilai muṉṉaṟivikkum.
(10)	mātavar nōṉpum maṭavār kaṟpum, kāvalaṉ kāval iṉṟeṉil illai.
(11)	makkaḷai naṉṉeṟippaṭuttuvatu aracaṉ kaṭamai.
(12)	maḻai peyyāmaikkum viḷaivuk kuṟaiviṟkum iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa nikaḻccikkum, aracaṉ kāraṇam.
(13)	allaṟpaṭṭaḻuta kuṭikaḷiṉ kaṇṇīr aracaṉai aḻittuviṭum.
(14)	aṟam piḻaitta aracaṉukku aṟamē kūṟṟamākum.
(15)	kuṭi paḻitūṟṟutal aracaṉukku mika iḻivu.
(16)	aracar teyvaṅkaḷai ikaḻntāl avar nāṭṭiṟku aḻivu varum.
(17)	aracarāl avamatikkappaṭṭa muṉivarum, pulavarum capikkiṉ aracum nakarum aḻiyum.
(18)	teyvaṅkaṭkup paṭaikkiṉum paliyiṭiṉum maḻaipeytu nāṭu ceḻikkum.
(19)	araca vāḻkkai tuṉpam niṟaintatu (ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ karuttu).
(20)	nāṭukaḷiṟ ciṟantatu tamiḻnāṭu (tamiḻaracar karuttu).
(21)	muttamiḻ nāṭukaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pāṇṭināṭu (pūtappāṇ ṭiyaṉ karuttu).
(22)	araciṉmaiyum vēṟṟaracum nāṭṭuk kuṟṟamākum.
(23)	aracaṉ eppaṭi, kuṭikaḷ appaṭi.
(24)	aracaṉ kuṟṟam kuṭikaḷaiyum kuṭikaḷ kuṟṟam aracaṉaiyum cārum.
(25)	aracaṉ piṟa nāṭukaḷai velvatu kuṇamēyaṉṟik kuṟṟamaṉṟu.
(26)	oruvaṉ ceyta allatu ceyvitta kolaikku avaṉi ṭattu  maṭṭumaṉṟi avaṉ kulattāriṭattum paḻi vāṅka lām. aḵtu oru teyvattiṉ kōpattait taṇippatākak karutappaṭiṉ.
(27)	koḷḷaic cottum paṟimutalum kōyiṟkuc cērkkap
peṟiṉ tītillai.
(28)	pōril ceyta tīviṉaikaṭkuk kōyiṟ ṟiruppaṇikaḷ kaḻuvāy.
(29)	cittarum, muṉivarum pōṉṟa periyārait tuṇaik kōṭal aracark karaṇceyyum; avaraip piḻaittal avarukku aḻivu tarum.
23
nampaṟkariya ceytikaḷ
paḻantamiḻāṭciyil nikaḻntaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa pala ceytikaḷ, ikkālattāl nampaṟkariyaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu:
(1)	kaḻumalattil aviḻttuviṭṭa yāṉai, karikālaṉait tēṭic ceṉṟu karuvūriṟ kaṇṭu tāṉē taṉmītu ēṟṟik koṇṭu vantatu.
(2)	ūrkkaṇakkaṉ āṇṭiṟutiyil kāycciya, irumpēntik kaṇakkoppuvittatu.
(3)	karikālaṉ mukariyeṉṉum mukkaṇṇaṉiṉ mikaik kaṇṇaip paṭattilaḻikka, an noṭiyē am mukariyiṉ kaṇ tāṉē maṟaintatu.
(4)	pāmpukkuṭamum,paḻukkak kāycciya irumpum, aṟamaṉṟaṅ kaḷil teyvaccāṉṟāka uṇmaiyuṇartti vantatu.
(5)	pukāriluḷḷa catukkappūtam, aṟaipōkamaiccarum poyk kariyāḷarum uḷḷiṭṭa aṟuvakaik kuṟṟavāḷi kaḷaip   puṭaittuṇṭatu.
(6)	vārttikaṉai neṭuñceḻiyaṉ atikārikaḷ ciṟaiyiliṭṭa vuṭaṉ, maturaik kāḷikōyil tāṉē cāttikkoṇṭatu.
(7)	mūṉṟāṅ kulōttuṅkaḷ oru pirāmaṇaṉukkuk kolait taṇṭaṉai vitikka, at taṇṭaṉai niṟaivēṟṟappaṭṭa piṉ, ap pirāmaṇaṉ āvi iravum pakalum aracaṉait toṭarntu tuṉpuṟutti, iṟutiyil tiruviṭaimarutūril nīṅkiṉatu.
24
aṉṟāṭa nikaḻcci
aracar vāḻkkaiyil aṉṟāṭa nikaḻccikaḷ potuvākap piṉvarumāṟu nikaḻntuvantaṉa:
vaikaṟaiyil, akavar (cūtar) aracaṉiṉ paḷḷi māṭattiṟku veḷiyē vāyilaruku niṉṟu, avaṉ muṉṉōriṉ pukaḻai iṉitākap pāṭi avaṉait tuyiluṇarttuvar.
acaraṉ tuyiluṇarntu, aṉṟu ceyyavēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic cintittup pularum vēḷaiyiṟ paḷḷiviṭṭeḻuvaṉ. avaṉ eḻun tamaikkaṟikuṟiyākak kālai muracam eṉṉum paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam aṭikkappeṟum.
aracaṉ eḻuntapiṉ, paṭaikkalap payiṟciyiruppiṉ ataic ceytuviṭṭuk kālaikkaṭaṉkaḷai muṭittu nīrāṭi iṟaivaḻipāṭu muṟṟik kālaiyuṇavuṇṭu, ōlakka maṇṭapattiṟkuc celvaṉ.
aṅkuk koṭaimuracu aṭikkappaṭum. palvēṟiṭattiṉiṉ ṟum, vantirukkum, pulavar pāṇar kūttar mutaliya paricilar, pāṭiyum āṭiyum tattam tiṟamaikāṭṭi, ataṟkut taka aracaṉiṭam paricu peṟuvar.
koṭai nikaḻtti muṭintavuṭaṉ, muṟaimuracu aṭikkappaṭum. vaḻakkāḷikaḷ tattam vaḻakkaic colvataṟku muṉṉarē vantiruppar.  muracaṭittavuṭaṉ vaḻakkuk kēṭkai toṭaṅkum.
aracaṉ ōlakkamaṇṭapattiliruntu koṭai nikaḻtti muṭinta piṉ, avvak kāla viṉainilaimaik kēṟpac cūḻviṉai maṇṭapat tiṟkēṉum aṟamaṉṟattiṟkēṉum celliṉum cellalām; aṉṟi ōlakka maṇṭapattilēyē iruntu taṉṉāṭ ṭeḻuttup pōkku varattaiyum ayalnāṭṭeḻuttup pōkkuvarattaiyum kavaṉip piṉum kavaṉikkalām.
iv viṉaikaḷellām muṟpakal nikaḻccikaḷākum.
naṇpakalil aracaṉ uṇavuṇṭapiṉ iraṇṭoru nāḻikai allatu veyil taṇiyumvarai iḷaippāṟalām. ataṉpiṉ mīṇṭum ōlakkamaṇṭapam  ceṉṟu ciṟitu nēram araciyaṟ kāriyaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkalām.
piṟpakal nikaḻccikaḷ potuvāka, aracarkkuk kuṭumpak kāriyak kavaṉippākavum, iṉpap poḻutupōkkākavumē yirukkum.
irāvuṇavu uṇṭapiṉ, ciṟitunēram uraiyāṭiyiruntu, ūraṭaṅkum vēḷaiyil aracaṉ paḷḷikoḷvaṉ. paḷḷimāṭat tiṟkup paḷḷimaṇṭapam, paḷḷiyampalam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
kārkālattilum, pōrkkālattilum, viḻānāḷilum nāṭu kāvaṟ cuṟṟuppōkku nāḷkaḷilum, piṟa ciṟappu viṉainikaḻcci yiṉpōtum, mēṟkūṟiya aṉṟāṭa nikaḻcci viṉaikaḷ māṟiyum nikaḻum.
potuvāka, aṟattai uyirnāṭiyākak koṇṭirunta ceṅkōlaracarellām, evviṭattum ennērattum, perumuṟai kēṭāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷaik kavaṉittaṟku āyattamāyiruntatākavē terikiṉṟatu.
"talaimakaṉāl talainiṉṟu oḻukappaṭuvaṉa tarumam aruttam kāmam eṉa mūṉṟu; am mūṉṟiṉaiyum oru pakalai mūṉṟu kūṟiṭṭu mutaṟkaṭ pattu nāḻikaiyum aṟattoṭu paṭaccellum, iṭaiyaṉa pattu nāḻikaiyum, aruttattoṭu paṭaccellum; kaṭaiyaṉa pattu nāḻikaiyum kāmattoṭu paṭac cellum; ātalāṉ talaimakaṉ mutaṟkaṇ pattunāḻikai tarumattoṭu paṭuvāṉ; talaimakaḷum vēṇṭavē tāṉum vēṇṭip pōntu attāṇi pukuntu aṟaṅ kēṭpatum aṟattoṭu paṭac colvatuñ ceyyum, nāḻikai aḷantukoṇṭu iṭaiyaṉa pattu nāḻikaiyum iṟaiyum muṟaiyuṅ kēṭṭu aruttattiṉoṭu paṭṭaṉavē ceytu vāḻvāṉām. av varuttattu nīkkattuk kaṭaiyaṉa pattu nāḻikaiyil talaimakaḷuḻaip pōtarum," eṉpatu iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai  (pa. 226).
aracarkkuk kālaikkaṭaṉ kaḻittalum, ulakattiliruntu nāṭu kāvaṟṟoḻil celuttalum, viruntuṭaṉ aṭicil kaitoṭa lum, nāvalaroṭu kalvi payiṟalum, āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟ kaḷittalum, maṭavāroṭu kūṭalum, tuyiṟalum, tuyiluṇarntu tēvarp parāvalu mākiya kālavarai eṭṭu, eṉpatu tañcaivāṇaṉ kōvaiyurai (pa. 248).
25
illaṟa vāḻkkai
mūvēntarum, perunila maṉṉar kuṭiyilum kuṟunila maṉṉar kuṭiyilum vēḷir kuṭiyilum peṇ koṇṭaṉar. avarukkup pala tēviyar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ talaiyāṉ kōpperuntēvikku aracaṉukkuḷḷa matippiruntatu. ōlakka maṇṭapattilum cūḻviṉaik kūṭṭattilum nakarvalattilum, kōpperuntēviyum aracaṉoṭu kūṭa amarntiruppatu vaḻakkam.
tēvimāruḷ aracaṉāl ciṟappāka kātalikkappaṭupavaḷ kāmakkiḻatti eṉappaṭuvāḷ. oruvarkkē urimai pūṇuṅ kulap parattaimakaḷāyk kāmaṅkāraṇamākat talaimakaṉāl varaintu koḷḷappaṭṭavaḷ kāmakkiḻatti eṉa, nampiyakap poruḷ kūṟum. utayakumaraṉ maṇimēkalaiyai ivvakaiyil maṇakka virumpiyiruk kalām.
caṅkakālat taracar cilar cērip parattaiyaroṭum toṭarpu koṇṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. parattaiyar cēriyiluḷḷa makaḷirkkuc cēripparattaiyar eṉṟum, avaruḷ aracaṉāl kātalikkappaṭupavaṭkuk kātaṟ parattai yeṉṟum, avaṉāl varaintukoḷḷappaṭṭa parattaikku iṟparattai yeṉṟum peyar. aracaṉ toṭarpukoḷḷum parattaiyarellām, oruvarkkē yurimai pūṇuṅ kulap parattaiyārākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aracaṉāṟ kātalikkappaṭum parattaiyarellām, avaṉu rimaiyeṉa avaṉ peṟṟōrālēyē otukkappaṭṭu iḷamaimutal vaḷarkkappaṭṭavar eṉṟu, iṟaiyaṉā rakapporuḷuraiyāciriyar kūṟuvar (pak. 226-pa.8.)
iṉi, aracaṉ vēṭṭaikkum ulāviṟkum ceṉṟaviṭattu yārēṉum ōraḻakiya aracakaṉṉiyait taṉiyiṭattuk kāṇiṉ, avaḷaik kaḷavumaṇam purivatumuṇṭu. neṭumuṭikkiḷḷi nāka nāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷākiya pīlivaḷaiyaik kaṭaṟkaraiyiṟ kaṇṭu kaḷavumaṇam  purintatāka, maṇimēkalai kūṟum. iyalumiṭamel lām kaḷavu kaṟpākat toṭarum. aracaṉ maṇattiṟku ittuṇai yeṉa oru varampillātiruntatu.
araṇmaṉaiyil makaḷir uṟaivataṟkut taṉiyiṭamuṇṭu. aḵtu uvaḷakam eṉappaṭum. atil aracaṉaiyum iḷavaracaraiyun tavirap paruvamaṭainta āṭavar vēṟu yārum pukuvatillai. kuṟaḷaruñ cintarum peṇṭirum cōṉakap pēṭiyarum vāḷēnti uvaḷakattaik kākkumāṟu amarttappaṭṭiruntaṉar. kaṉṉiyar uṟaivataṟkut taṉimāṭam iruntatu. atu kaṉṉikaimāṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ uvaḷakañ cellum pōtellām makaḷir parivāramē uṭaṉ cellum. tēviyarukkut taṉittaṉi māḷikai yiruntatu.
aracaṉ vēṟoruttiyaik kātalittāṉ eṉṟō vēṟu kāraṇampaṟṟiyō, kōpperuntēvi ūṭiyirukkumpōtu, pulavar pāṇar mutaliyōr vāyilāy avaḷ ūṭalait tīrppatu aracaṉ vaḻakkam.
aracaṉukkum aracikkum, aṭimaiyarum tōḻiyarum kuṟṟēva larum parivārattārum, meykāvalarumākap paṟpala ūḻiyar iruntaṉar. araci cellumiṭamellām parivāra makaḷir eṇ maṅkala (aṭṭamaṅkala)p poruḷkaḷai ēntic celluvatu vaḻakkam.
nakaiccuvai yuṇarccimikka aracar, tam conta iṉpattiṟ keṉṟu oru nakaiyāṇṭiyai amarttikkoḷvatu muṇṭu. avaṉukkuk kōmāḷi allatu kōṇaṅki eṉṟu peyar.
araṇmaṉai vēlaikkeṉṟu taṉiyāka, aivakaik kammi yarum, mayirviṉaiñaṉ vaṇṇāṉ cemmāṉ mutaliya kuṭimak kaḷum, piṟa toḻilāḷarum amarttappeṟṟiruntaṉar. avarai araṇmaṉait taṭṭāṉ araṇmaṉai mayirviṉaiñaṉ araṇ maṉaik kuyavaṉ eṉa aḻaippatu vaḻakkam. araṇmaṉaip poṟ kollaṉ caṭṭaiyaṇintiruntāṉ eṉṟu cilappatikāraṅ kūṟuva tāl, aracaṉoṭu neruṅkip paḻakupavarkkellām ac ciṟappaḷik kappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
aracaṉukku uṭpakaiyum aṟaipōku viṉaiñarumpaṟṟiya accamirukkumpōtu, araṇmaṉai maruttuvar avaṉ uṇavai aṉṉam cakakaravākam karuṅkuraṅku mutaliya uyirkaḷaikkoṇṭu nōṭṭañ ceyvar. aracaṉ uṇṭapiṉ avaṉukku veṟṟilai maṭittuk koṭuppavaṉukku aṭaippaiyāṉ (aṭaippaik kāraṉ) eṉṟu peyar.
araṇmaṉaic celavellām araciyaṟ celavākavē karutap paṭṭatu. araciyaṟ celavum araṇmaṉaic celavum pōka eñciya potuvakai (cātāraṇa)p poruḷkaḷellām, koṭaikkup payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aracarkkē takum aruvilaiyaṇikalaṉ kaḷum, perumaṇikaḷum, peruntokaiyāṉa poṟkācu kaḷum, aracaṉ conta uṭaimaiyākap pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟippiṭṭa niṟaikku mēṟpaṭṭa maṇikkaṟkaḷaik kuṭikaḷ vaittirukkavāvatu nāṭu kaṭattavāvatu kūṭāteṉṟum, aracaṉiṭattil oppuvittu vilaipeṟṟukkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟum kaṇṭippāṉa aracaviti yiruntatu. aracaṉukkuc contamāka irunta nilattiṟkuk kaṇṭuḻavu eṉṟu peyar. araciyarkkuc ciṟupāṭṭuc celaviṟkeṉṟu nilaṅkaḷum ūrkaḷum viṭappaṭṭu iruntatākavum terikiṉṟatu.
aracaṉ iṉpamākap poḻutupōkkuvataṟkup pala vaḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. kaṇpōlum naṇparōṭu aḷavaḷāvalum, ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ uyarnta kēḷvip pulavaroṭu payilalum, nakaiyāṇṭi coṟkēṭṭalum, vaṭṭāṭṭu, vallāṭṭu, puṉalāṭṭu mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷai yāṭalum ilavantikaic cōlaikkum ceykuṉṟiṟkum neytalaṅkāṉalukkum ulāp pōtalum, pāṇar pāṭiṉiyar kaṇikaiyariṉ icai kēṭṭalum, viṟaliyar kaṇikaiyar cākkaiyariṉ kūttuk kāṇṭalum, vēṭṭaiyāṭalum, malaivaḷaṅ kāṇalum, piṟavum aracaṉ iṉpamākap poḻutupōkkum vaḻikaḷām. aracaṉ ulāppōkum pōtu tōḻiyaroṭu celvatu vaḻakkam.1  avarukku aracaṉoṭu meytoṭṭup payilavum viḷaiyāṭavum urimaiyuṇṭu.
vaiyamuñ civikaiyu maṇikkā lamaḷiyum
 uyyā ṉatti ṉuṟutuṇai makiḻcciyuñ
 cāmaraik kavariyun tamaṉiya vaṭaippaiyuṅ
 kūrnuṉai vāḷuṅ kōmakaṉ koṭuppap
 peṟṟa celvam piṟaḻā vāḻkkaip
 poṟṟoṭi maṭantaiyar	               (14.126-131)
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakutiyāl aracaṉ parattaiyaroṭu ulāppōtalum, avarkku avaṉ paṟpala varicaiyaḷittalum, uṇṭeṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
aracar, paruvamvanta putalvarait tuṇaiyaraiyarāka allatu maṇṭalat talaivarāka, vevvēṟiṭat tamartti vaippatu perum
pāṉmai. avar tam putalviyarai araca kuṭiyiṉarkkaṉṟip piṟarkkuk koṭuppatillaiyeṉpatu, 3ām kulōttuṅkaṉ ampikā patiyaik koṉṟatiṉāl aṟiyappaṭum.
nāṭukāval, cantu (pakaitaṇiviṉai), pōr, naṭparacar, viḻā mutaliya kāraṇampaṟṟi, aracaṉ taṉ tēviyariṭattiṉiṉṟu pirintupōvaṉ, pirivuk kālattil, iṉṟiyamaiyum aṇikaḻaṟṟa lum, meyyalaṅkariyāmaiyum, aḷavāka vuṇṭalum, nāḷeṇ ṇik kaḻittalum, makiḻātiruttalum. teyvattai vēṇṭalum, mīṭcik kālattil varavukaṇṭu makiḻtalum viruntayartalum tēviyar mēṟkoḷḷuñ ceyalkaḷām.
aracaṉ iṟappiṉ, tēviyarum uṭaṉ iṟappar; allatu uṭaṉ kaṭṭaiyēṟuvar; allatu kaimmai pūṇuvar. avar muṉṉiruvakaiyil iṟappatē perumpāṉmai.
26
aracar muṭivu
tamiḻaracar muṭivu taṉippaṭṭatum kuṭippaṭṭatum eṉa iruvakai.
aracar taṉimuṭivu: tamiḻaracar, pōril iṟattalaiyē, perum pēṟākavum,viṇṇulakam pukum vaḻiyākavuṅ koṇṭiruntaṉar. ataṉāl, iyalumpōtellām avarum cēṉaiyuṭaṉ pōrukkuc celluvatu vaḻakkam. pōrilaṉṟi nōyālum mūppālum iṟakkum aracar vāḷpōḻntaṭakkap peṟuvar. ataṉ iyalpai,
nōyppāl viḷinta yākkai taḻīik
 kātaṉ maṟantavar tītumaruṅ kaṟumār
 aṟampuri koḷkai nāṉmaṟai mutalvar
 tiṟampuri pacumpuṟ parappiṉar kiṭappi
 maṟaṅkan tāka nallamar vīḻnta
 nīḷkaḻaṉ maṟavar celvuḻic celkeṉa
 vāḷpōḻn taṭakkal		(puṟam. 93: 5-11)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭup pakutiyāl aṟika. caṅkak kālattiṟkup piṟkālattil, vāḷpōḻntaṭakkal viṭappaṭṭatu.
pōriṟ peṟṟa puṟappuṇ nāṇiyum, putalvar taṉṉoṭu poravarukai nāṇiyum, piṟavakaiyil māṉakkēṭu nērntaviṭattum, ulakappaṟṟait tuṟantaviṭattum, aracar ūrukku vaṭakkil uṇṇānōṉpiruntu uyir tuṟappar. atu vaṭakkiruttal eṉappaṭum. pakaivarāl ciṟaippaṭṭiruntu uṇṇātiṟattalum vaṭakkiruttaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
vāḻnāḷiṟutiyil, aracāṭciyai iḷavaracaṉiṭam oppuvittu viṭṭuk kāṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu tavañceytal, orōvōr aracaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa ceyalākum.
aracarkkup piṟappiṉum, iṟappē ciṟappātalāl, koṟṟa aṭaimoḻiyillāta aracarellām, avar iṟanta iṭappeya rāl vēṟupaṭuttik kūṟappaṭṭaṉar.
aracariṟantapiṉ, avaruṭampait tāḻiyāṟ kavittalum, tāḻiyāṟ kavittup putaittalum, veṟumaṉē putaittalum, muṟkāla vaḻakkam; erittal piṟkāla vaḻakkam. aracarukku iṟutic caṭaṅkiyaṟṟal paḷḷipaṭuttal (paḷḷipaṭai) eṉṟum, avar niṉaivukkuṟiyāka eḻuppappaṭuṅ kaṭṭaṭam paḷḷippaṭaik kōyil eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.
aracakkuṭi muṭivu: muttamiḻaracarum tamiḻālum maṇavuṟa vālum iṇaikkappaṭṭu oṟṟumaiyāyirunta varai, acōkap pēraracaṉālum, avarai acaikka muṭiyavillai. caṅkakālattiṟkup piṟkālattil, tamiḻ pēṇāmai oṟṟumaiyiṉmai kulappiriviṉai mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl, muttamiḻ nāṭum mūvēṟu pirintu valimai kuṉṟippōyc cōḻapāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷ mutalāvatu 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tilli makamatiyap paṭaikaṭkum piṉpu 16ām   nūṟṟāṇṭil vicayanakara vaṭukap paṭaikaṭkum eḷitāy iraiyāyiṉa. pāṇṭināṭṭu valimai ataṉ tāyap pōrkaḷālum tēyntu māyntatu. iṟutippāṇṭiyaṉ ki. pi. 1652 varai iruntāṉ.
cērar kuṭiyil iṟutiyāka virunta aracar valimai yaṟṟavarā yiruntatiṉāl, cēranāṭāṉatu. vēṇāṭu (tiruvitaṅkōṭu)kocci kōḻikkōṭu kōlattunāṭu mutaliya pala ciṟu taṉiyaracukaḷākap pirintupōṉatuṭaṉ, 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭacoṟ kalappāl malaiyāḷa nāṭākavum tirintuviṭṭatu.
iṅṅaṉam, paṭaippuk kālantoṭṭu mēmpaṭṭu vantavai yeṉap pārāṭṭappeṟṟa mūvēntar kuṭikaḷum, 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭi ṭaiyil muṭivuṟṟaṉa.
piṉṉiṇaippu
1. makkaḷ nilaimai
  tamiḻaracar kālattil,  tamiḻ makkaḷ, tiṇainilai tiṇai mayakkanilai ākiya irunilaippaṭṭu vāḻntu vantaṉar. kuṟiñciyil kuṟavarum mullaiyil iṭaiyarum marutattil uḻavarum neytalil cempaṭavarum pālaiyil kaḷḷar maṟavar mutaliyōrumāka ovvoru tiṇaik kulamum taṉittaṉi vāḻvatu tiṇainilai; ciṟṟūrilum pērurilum nakarilum mānakarilum pala tiṇaikkulam kalantu vāḻvatu tiṇai mayakka nilai.
mutalāvatu marutanilattilum piṉṉarp piṟa nilattūrkaḷilum tiṇaimayakka nilaiyālum toḻiṟpiriviṉā lum pala kulaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭiruntaṉa.  āciriyarum pūcāriyārum tuṟaviyarum antaṇar eṉṟum āṭciviṉai pūṇṭōr  aracar eṉṟum, viṟpaṉaiyum iruvakai vāṉikamum mēṟkoṇṭōr vaṇikar eṉṟum, uḻutum uḻuvittum uṇpōr vēḷāḷar  eṉṟum, kaittoḻil ceypavar viṉaivalar eṉṟum, kuṟṟēval ceypavar ēvalar eṉṟum, irantuṇpōr irappōr eṉṟum marutanilai makkaḷ eḻuperu vakuppiṉarāka vakukkappaṭṭu nālvakai nilattum nāṭu muḻutum paraviṉar.
muṟkūṟiyavāṟu perunakaraṅkaḷil vantu taṅkiya ayal nāṭṭār,  nāṭupaṟṟiya kulattiṉarāy vāḻntu vantaṉar.
makkaḷellārum perumpālum muṉṉōr toḻilaiyē ceytu vantālum, avarukku virumpiya toḻilai mēṟkoḷḷa urimai yiruntatu.  piṟappuppaṟṟiya kulappirivum, ap pirivupaṟṟiya ēṟṟat tāḻvum, tīṇṭāmaiyum muṟkālattilillai.  toḻil paṟṟiya kulappirivum, aṟivum tuppuravum oḻukkamumpaṟṟiya ēṟṟattāḻvumē iruntuvantaṉa.  kalvi ellārukkum potu vāyiruntatu.  pāṇar icai nāṭakat toḻilaiyum, vaḷḷuvara kaṇiyat toḻilaiyum maḻiviṉaiñar  celliyat toḻilaiyum (Surgery), eṅkum naṭatti vantaṉar.
karumpu tiṉṉak kaikkūli  koṭukkavum, kuḻaikoṇṭu kōḻi yeṟiyavum viḷaivuñ celvamum viñciyiruntaṉa.
aṟivum aruḷuñcāṉṟa antaṇarum tammaiyun tam putalvaraiyum muṟaiceyyum aracarum,
vaṭuvañci vāymoḻintu
 tamavum piṟavum oppa nāṭik
koḷvatūu mikaikoḷātu koṭuppatū uṅ kuṟai koṭātu
palpaṇṭam pakarntuvīcum1 		(paṭṭiṉappālai, 208-211)
vaṇikarum, viruntirukka vuṇṇāta vēḷāḷarum palki yiruntaṉar.
poruḷātārattai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu uḻavaṉaiyē talaimaik kuṭivāṇaṉākap paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭu pōṟṟivantatu.  makkaḷiṭai oṟṟumaiyum aracaṉukkum kuṭikaṭkum iṭaiyē irutalaiyaṉpum iruntuvantaṉa.  aracaṉāṇai eṉṟa toṭar moḻikkuk kūṭa aṉaivarum kaṭṭuppaṭṭiruntaṉar.
piṟappuppaṟṟiya kulappirivu tamiḻnāṭṭil ēṟpapaṭṭati liruntu,  tamiḻnāṭṭu nilaimāṟiviṭṭatu, tamiḻarukkuḷ kulappaṟṟu mikuntu iṉavuṇarcci (National feeling) kuṉṟippōyiṟṟu.  tīṇṭāmaiyālum atiṉiluṅ koṭiya kāṇāmaiyālum tākkuṇṭa orucārt tamiḻiṉam taṉ cīriya nilaimaiyaiyum, makkaḷurimaiyaiyum iḻantatu.  curuṅkac colliṉ, tamiḻiṉam muḻutum palvēṟuvakaiyil ciṉṉa piṉṉappaṭṭut tāḻvaṭaintu viṭṭatu.
aṭikkaṭi nikaḻnta pōrālum, iṭaiyum iṟutiyum ēṟpaṭṭa ayalār āṭciyālum, paṭaiyēṟu kuḻappattālum. koḷḷaiyālum, veḷḷattālum, pañcattālum, varik koṭumaiyālum, potumakkaḷ nilaimai varavarac cīr keṭṭu vantatu.  vaṟumaiyum aṭimaittaṉamum vaḷarntōṅkiṉa.  taṉiyaṭi maiyum kottaṭimaiyum kulavaṭimaiyumākap paṟpalar mīḷā aṭimaippaṭṭu, avar vaḻiyiṉarum an nilaiyarāyiṉar.  veḷiyār tiraḷtiraḷāy vantu kuvintatiṉālum, makkaḷ tokai mikkatiṉā lum, poruḷātāra vītaṅ kuṉṟiṟṟu.  kuṭikaḷ valacai pōṉatiṉāl cila ūrkaḷ kuṭippāḻāyiṉa.  aṉpāṭci nīṅki atikāravāṭci ōṅkiyatāl, kuṭikaḷ perumpālum añci vāḻa vēṇṭiyatā yiṟṟu.  makkaḷiṭaiyē oṟṟumaiyum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum aracap paṟṟum maṟaintaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam, tamiḻāṭci muṭivuṟṟa 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻakam, palavakaiyilum alaippuṇṭu allaṟpaṭṭiruntatu.
2.moḻi nilaimai
mutaṟkālattil, tamiḻ ellāt tuṟaikaḷilum taṉiyāṭci celutti muḻuttūymaiyāy vaḻaṅki vantatu.  āyiṉ, iṭaik kālattil vaṭamoḻi tēvapāṭai eṉṟu mūvēntarālum oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭatiṉāl purōkitar naṭattum pūcai karaṇaṅkaṭ kellām tamiḻ vilakkappaṭṭatu.  vaṭacol vaḻaṅkuvatu uyarvu eṉṉuṅ karuttum nāḷaṭaivil eḻuntatu.  tamiḻaracarum orucila tamiḻppulavarum tamiḻaip puṟakkaṇikkavun talaippaṭṭaṉar.
toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻccaṅkam irīit tamiḻai vaḷarttut tamiḻnāṭaṉ  eṉṟu  ciṟappikkap peṟṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭiyiṉarē, ki.pi. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil caṅkattaik kulaittuviṭṭaṉar.  ataṉ piṉ, 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ataip putuppikkumāṟu poyyā moḻip pulavar ceyta perumuyaṟciyum vīṇāyppōyiṟṟu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē, tamiḻil iṭampeṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉa.  āyiṉ, avar kālattil taṟpava mākavum citaivākavum orōvoṉṟāy arukiyē vaḻaṅkiṉa.  piṟkālattilō, avai paṭippaṭiyāyp peruki vantatuṭaṉ, kaṭaic caṅkattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattil, potumakkaṭ keṉṟēṟpaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷil taṟcamamākavum kirantaveḻutti lum tōṉṟi, kalveṭṭut tamiḻaiyē tamiḻarkku viḷaṅkātatum piḻai malintatumāṉa maṇippavaḻak kalavai moḻivaḻiyāka māṟṟiviṭṭaṉa. aracar peyar1 teyvap peyar iṭappeyar mutaliya palvakaip peyarkaḷum nāḷaṭaivil vaṭacoṟkaḷāy māṟiṉa.
